Motivation: 现有的3D分子生成扩散模型缺乏判断生成分子质量高低的原则性信号，无法识别何时生成的分子可能质量较差。

Method: 基于去噪网络的Laplace近似，测量噪声预测在生成轨迹上的变异性，构建不确定性分数作为样本质量指示器。

Result: 不确定性分数与已有的样本级质量指标呈负相关，能有效预测样本质量；利用该分数过滤生成样本可在测试时通过缩放提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为分子扩散模型提供了有效的质量评估和筛选机制，实现了测试时缩放，提升了生成样本的整体质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [112] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将神经网络模型编辑建模为强化学习问题，通过智能体学习编辑策略，在机器遗忘和偏见缓解任务上取得了显著成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练神经网络编辑算法需要针对特定目标进行专门设计，这一过程耗时且需要大量人力投入。论文旨在探索一种通用框架，避免为每个编辑任务手动设计专门的算法。

Method: 提出一个探索性框架，将神经模型编辑形式化为强化学习问题。设计了两个环境：MaskWorld（乘法权重缩放）和ShiftWorld（加法权重更新）。奖励函数结合了效用保留目标和特定任务编辑目标，使智能体能够在学习目标化修改的同时保持模型整体性能。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务中，学习到的策略将遗忘集准确率降至近0%，同时保持超过90%的保留集准确率。在偏见缓解任务中，学习到的策略将偏见相关性能提升超过5%，同时保持了整体分类效用。

Conclusion: 神经模型编辑可以被建模为强化学习问题，编辑策略可以从奖励反馈中学习得到，而无需针对每个任务进行人工设计，这为模型编辑提供了一种新的通用方法。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [113] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MaxProof，一个针对竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，使M3模型在IMO 2025和USAMO 2026上超越人类金牌门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛级数学证明对大语言模型具有挑战性，需要提升测试时的推理能力以解决复杂证明问题。

Method: M3模型训练三种面向证明的能力：证明生成、证明验证和基于评论的证明修复，采用低假阳率的防御式生成验证器。测试时，MaxProof将模型作为生成器、验证器、优化器和排序器，在候选证明群体上搜索，通过锦标赛选择返回最终证明。

Result: M3模型在IMO 2025上达到35/42分，在USAMO 2026上达到36/42分，两项均超过人类金牌门槛。

Conclusion: MaxProof框架通过测试时扩展显著提升了数学证明能力，验证了群体级搜索在复杂推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [114] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个超分子化学基准SupraBench，包含四个核心任务和一个辅助视觉任务，并构建了1600万词元的领域语料库SupraPMC。评估发现现有LLM在超分子化学推理任务上存在显著提升空间，不同任务族呈现不同的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分子化学中的主客体系统设计耗时且需要大量实验验证。尽管LLM在分子结合任务上展现出潜力，但缺乏系统性评估LLM在超分子化学推理能力（如结合亲和力预测等基础任务）的基准。为填补这一空白，作者与领域专家合作构建了首个超分子化学基准。

Method: 1）设计四个基础任务：结合亲和力预测、最佳结合体选择、溶剂识别和主客体描述，外加分子识别的辅助视觉任务；2）构建SupraPMC语料库，从Europe PMC精选1600万词元的超分子化学文章；3）在广泛的开源和专有LLM上进行基准测试；4）开展领域自适应预训练实验。

Result: 1）所有LLM在各任务上均存在显著提升空间；2）基于SupraPMC的领域自适应预训练能有效迁移到分布内回归任务，但在严格字母格式输出上存在权衡；3）不同任务族的难度特征差异明显，揭示了当前超分子化学推理中特定的能力缺陷。

Conclusion: SupraBench首次系统性评估了LLM在超分子化学领域的推理能力，揭示了现有模型在结合亲和力预测等核心任务上的局限性。不同任务族的差异化失败模式为未来改进指明了方向。该基准和语料库为超分子化学领域的LLM研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [115] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRAFTIIF框架，首次统一处理多变量时间序列的四类结构异常（点、分布、时间、集体），在mTSBench基准上VUS-PR达0.463（较先前最优+40.7%），并提供天然可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测面临四类结构不同异常的挑战（点异常/孤立尖峰、分布异常/水平偏移、时间异常/节奏变化、集体异常/传感器间相关性破坏），现有无监督方法通常仅针对一两类异常，且可解释性有限，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 提出CRAFTIIF（跨分辨率分析四类型可解释隔离森林）框架：(1)生成K=500个随机分析小波特征提取，覆盖四族小波（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet），每族针对特定异常类型；(2)训练五个结构化隔离森林（四个类型特定IF + 一个元IF用于复合异常）；(3)采用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值在0.1%-69.2%异常率范围内自动校准检测。由于每个IF仅在类型特定特征上训练，分支触发天然提供异常类型归因，无需事后解释。

Result: 在mTSBench基准全部19个数据集上评估：平均F1=0.228（全部19个数据集），F1=0.322（13个可检测数据集）；VUS-PR达0.463，较先前最优0.329提升40.7%，在25个评估方法中排名第一。诊断框架揭示19个数据集中6个对任何无监督方法均不可检测。消融实验（11个条件）证实自适应阈值（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）、元IF（+23%）均不可或缺。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF成功实现了四类异常的统一检测与天然可解释性，无需数据集特定调参，在基准测试中取得最优性能。框架提供的诊断工具揭示了无监督方法的根本可检测性限制，为未来研究提供了重要见解。代码已开源。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [116] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现了任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，无需目标样本即可收敛到奖励倾斜分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任意长度离散扩散模型缺乏原则性的奖励引导微调方法，需要探索统一框架来优化生成质量。

Method: 推导联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，建立去掩码和插入质量作为最小化解码误差的可处理方法，引入自适应联合解码(AJD)损失函数，理论证明其产生最优路径测度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化方面表现优异，相比先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法，在生成灵活性和准确性上均有提升。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型提供了首个统一的理论框架，实现了无需目标样本的奖励引导微调，具有理论保证和实证效果。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [117] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了调整杯积神经网络层，通过引入来自高阶规范理论的调整项，构建了设计上即具有规范不变性的可观测张量积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理和几何中的许多重要可观测张量积是上链的杯积，需要构建具有规范不变性的神经网络原语来处理这些可观测张量积。

Method: 引入调整杯积神经网络层，将杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项硬连线结合，通过调整系数控制输出，证明其在闭链上的输出完全依赖于调整系数。

Result: 在闭链上，输出完全依赖于调整系数；将调整系数设为零会完全移除输出；该可观测张量积是非零二次型，在一次和二次规范变换下精确不变。

Conclusion: 调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，所提出的调整杯积神经网络层成功实现了设计上的规范不变性。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [118] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Timeflies框架，将缺失值时间序列预测重构为未来可观测性推断与数值估计的联合问题，并构建Shadow基准与OVJE评估指标，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如插值后预测、神经ODE）虽能建模历史不规则性，但在推理时依赖一个隐式oracle假设：预设未来有效观测的时间戳已知。这在现实中不合理，因为核心问题不仅是预测未来值，还需判断未来是否会出现有效观测。

Method: 提出Timeflies统一框架，采用观测流与数值流双流结构，通过可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导依赖建模和联合预测三个模块显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互；同时构建Shadow基准（结合公开数据集自然缺失性与工业数据）并提出Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE)评估指标。

Result: 在Shadow基准上的广泛实验表明，Timeflies在多个场景下持续优于现有方法，验证了显式建模未来可观测性对含缺失值时序预测的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式建模未来可观测性对于处理现实世界中高度不完整且不规则的时间序列预测至关重要，Timeflies框架通过联合推断观测与数值，有效解决了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [119] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“可模拟过程”框架，在强依赖数据下通过访问模拟器恢复了经典独立数据下的学习保证，并展示了条件采样在统计和计算上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习理论的核心问题是理解泛化所需的最小假设，但现有结果大多依赖数据的独立性或其代理，针对强依赖数据的成果非常有限，因此需要新的框架来突破这一限制。

Method: 引入可模拟过程框架，假设学习者可访问一个模拟器来近似数据生成分布（该分布可以是任意复杂且具强依赖的过程），并在此框架下分析学习保证与条件采样的能力。

Result: 在有模拟器访问的情况下，可恢复与经典独立数据设置相同的学习保证，即误差界依赖于VC维；证明了条件采样在统计和计算上具有严格优势；提出了一种单一算法，可在所有有界多项式时间可采样的过程下学习任意给定VC类，其遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 该框架显著拓宽了经典PAC模型的概念，为强依赖数据下的学习理论提供了新的理论基础和保证。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [120] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCSB框架，通过联合优化问题实现Bagging集成模型的剪枝与概率校准，在保持或提升精度的同时实现高达96%的压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bagging集成方法（如随机森林、Bagged SVM等）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，这种朴素假设忽略了基估计器局部能力的差异，导致模型过度自信且未考虑估计器间的性能差异。

Method: 将集成剪枝与校准建模为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，通过最小化Out-Of-Bag (OOB)损失进行优化。针对L1范数在单纯形上恒定无法实现剪枝的"L1-simplex悖论"，引入凹二次惩罚项来诱导稀疏性。

Result: 在多种模型上实现高达96%的集成压缩率，获得线性推理加速；同时实现更优的概率校准（降低Expected Calibration Error），并保持或提升泛化精度。

Conclusion: SCSB是一个模型无关的数学严谨框架，成功解决了Bagging集成的压缩与校准问题，证明了通过理论指导的优化方法可以有效提升集成模型的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过早期退出估计推理步骤的因果重要性，发现思维链存在“承诺边界”——模型在此处从中间猜测迅速转变为稳定答案，边界后的步骤对最终答案影响甚微。利用该特性可实现推理链长度平均缩减55%且几乎不影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链是推理时扩展的主导范式，但各步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不明确。研究旨在量化步骤重要性，揭示答案形成过程，从而优化推理效率。

Method: 通过早期退出估计每个步骤的因果重要性；使用注意力探针从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段；基于检测到的承诺边界实施推理块的早期退出。

Result: 推理通常经历“承诺边界”——从短暂中间猜测急剧转变为高置信稳定答案，常在单个步骤内完成且早于推理块结束；边界后的步骤为“副现象性”步骤，不影响最终答案概率；注意力探针能高精度解码并泛化到未见任务；早期退出平均缩短CoT长度55%，对性能影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 思维链存在可识别的答案形成转折点，边界后的步骤冗余；利用该信号可实现高效推理，减少不必要的计算，为推理时优化提供了理论与方法依据。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [122] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持续学习中“灾难性遗忘”主要是可访问性与流形对齐问题而非知识破坏，遗忘知识可在低维子空间中被紧凑恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点常将灾难性遗忘视为已学知识在序列学习中的破坏；基于可访问性崩溃框架，作者旨在从几何结构角度研究持续学习中的“可恢复性”问题，以澄清遗忘的本质。

Method: 使用Split CIFAR-100数据集和顺序训练的ResNet-18，在10个任务上分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度；提出恢复子空间维度(k_t)，定义为保持探针性能90%所需的最小奇异方向数；采用主角度度量表征漂移并建立几何模型。

Result: 恢复维度在训练中保持稳定（均值k_t=8.0），尽管表征发生显著漂移；主角度漂移与可恢复性强负相关(r=-0.862)；简单几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差。结果支持“稳定恢复流形”假说，表明被遗忘知识尽管经历表征重组，仍在低维子空间中保持可解码性。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘主要是一个可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。这为持续学习提供了新视角，指导未来方法应关注如何改善知识访问而非防止信息丢失。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [123] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了同策略蒸馏(OPD)的参数更新具有坐标稀疏性和特定的几何特征，仅训练发现的子网络即可恢复几乎全部性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏(OPD)作为重要的后训练方法，结合了同策略学生轨迹和密集教师监督，但其如何改变模型参数的机制尚不明确，需要深入研究其参数更新的特性。

Method: 在多个语言模型和视觉-语言模型对及应用场景中，从稀疏性和几何特性两个维度分析OPD风格更新的参数变化特征，并通过消融实验比较SGD和AdamW优化器的表现。

Result: 稀疏性方面：OPD更新小且坐标稀疏，分布跨层且主要集中在FFN，仅训练发现的子网络可恢复接近完整OPD的性能。几何方面：更新数值上满秩但谱集中，主要远离源权重的奇异主子空间，且不成比例地落在源权重接近零的坐标上。AdamW因能处理密集教师监督保留的异构坐标梯度尺度而优于SGD。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未将OPD转变为普通的密集参数重写；相反，OPD保留了同策略后训练的重要几何特征，这为理解和改进蒸馏方法提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [124] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对截断位置编码（PEs）进行理论研究，发现截断后不同PEs家族的表达能力存在根本差异：截断谱PEs不再强于1-WL测试，且混合使用截断PEs在实际数据集上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为谱PEs和游走PEs具有相同的表达能力（介于1-WL和3-WL之间），但该结论仅在使用完整PEs（O(n³)复杂度）时成立。实践中普遍采用截断PEs（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的幂），然而这些截断PEs的理论性质尚不清楚，需要填补这一理论空白。

Method: 从理论上分析截断PEs的表达能力差异；证明截断谱PEs的表达能力上限；研究k-调和距离谱PEs家族以揭示相近截断PEs的表达能力差异；在真实数据集上对混合截断PEs进行实验验证。

Result: 证明截断后不同PEs家族的表达能力存在根本差异，不再等价；作为推论，证明截断谱PEs不再强于1-WL测试；揭示相近截断PEs间也存在表达能力差异；实验表明混合使用截断PEs比单一家族效果更好。

Conclusion: 截断操作从根本上改变了不同位置编码家族的理论性质，打破了原有的表达能力等价关系。这要求实践者在选择PEs时需谨慎考虑截断的影响，并建议采用混合策略以获得更好的性能。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Free-Placement Optimization of Ground Station Locations for Low-Earth Orbit Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12667)
*Grace Ra Kim,Duncan Eddy,Vedant Srinivas,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCORE方法，用于地面站网络的自由选址优化，相比传统固定选址方法提升最大15%的下行链路吞吐量，收敛速度提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速扩张的低轨卫星星座对地面网络提出了更高要求，现有方法仅能从预设位置中选择站点，限制了优化空间和性能，亟需更高效的地面站网络设计方法。

Method: 提出SCORE（Sequential Cyclic Optimization via Refinement & Evaluation）两阶段自由选址优化方法，结合序列坐标选择与循环精炼策略，解决高维、非凸和局部最优等挑战。

Result: 在Capella Space、ICEYE及Walker-Star星座测试中，SCORE相比差分进化算法减少5倍函数评估次数，下行吞吐量提升最高13%；相比固定选址方法，无约束SCORE提升最大15%下行总量，基础设施约束版本保持超过92%增益。

Conclusion: SCORE方法在地面站网络自由选址优化中显著优于传统方法，为未来地面网络设计提供了性能基准，同时考虑了实际基础设施约束，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Rapidly expanding low Earth orbit satellite constellations are placing increasing demands on terrestrial ground networks, motivating the development of more efficient ground station network designs. Current approaches select sites from predefined locations, limiting optimization to existing infrastructure and constraining performance. In contrast, free-placement optimization operates over a continuous spatial domain on Earth, broadening the search space and allowing higher-throughput configurations at the cost of potentially requiring new infrastructure deployment. In this work, we introduce SCORE (Sequential Cyclic Optimization via Refinement & Evaluation), a two-stage free-placement method for ground station design. SCORE combines sequential coordinate selection with cyclic refinement to manage high-dimensionality, non-convexity, and local minima that challenge global optimizers. We benchmark SCORE against one-shot methods such as differential evolution (DE) and integer programming approaches using locations from Kongsberg Satellite Services and the World Teleport Association. Tests across two commercial Earth observation constellations (Capella Space and ICEYE) and one synthetic Walker-Star constellation show that SCORE requires up to 5x fewer function evaluations to converge relative to DE while improving downlink throughput by up to 13%. Compared to fixed-site methods, unconstrained SCORE achieves up to 15% greater total downlink, establishing a strong empirical performance benchmark for flexible placement; infrastructure-constrained SCORE retains over 92% of this gain while restricting placement to within proximity of existing fiber and power infrastructure. We also explore trade-offs between expanding existing stations and deploying new sites, informing future ground network design for operational constellations.

</details>


### [126] [ScaleAcross: Designing Multi-Data-Center Infrastructure for Geo-Distributed AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12963)
*Naved Inam,Aryan Alpesh Bhavsar,Masabattula Teja Nikhil,Sidharth Sharma*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于EVPN-VXLAN的可扩展仿真框架，用于在真实广域网条件下研究地理分布式AI训练的系统行为，并通过实验分析了AllReduce和参数服务器通信模式下的流量分布和基础设施性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型的快速增长和数据主权要求的提高，跨多个数据中心的地理分布式AI训练成为趋势。然而，这种部署面临着同步密集型通信、跨站点数据交换和广域网延迟约束等系统级挑战，缺乏系统性的研究框架来理解和解决这些问题。

Method: 提出一个基于EVPN-VXLAN的可扩展仿真框架，使用ContainerLab和FRRouting实现。该框架结合了VXLAN覆盖网络与基于EVPN的数据中心互联，集成了等价多路径路由（ECMP）、双向转发检测（BFD）以及队列感知的流量分布机制，以改善同步密集型AI工作负载的通信行为，同时保持与通用基础设施的兼容性。

Result: 通过真实WAN仿真实验，对采用AllReduce和参数服务器通信模式的分布式训练工作负载进行了特征分析，获得了关于地理分布式AI环境中流量分布、弹性和基础设施行为的深入见解。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可重现的多数据中心基础设施框架对于可扩展的分布式AI训练具有重要潜力，EVPN-VXLAN能够有效支撑地理分布式AI训练环境，为理解和优化此类系统提供了有价值的工具和洞察。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI models and increasing data sovereignty requirements are driving the transition toward geo-distributed AI training across multiple data centers. Such deployments introduce system-level challenges arising from synchronization-intensive communication, cross-site data exchange, and wide-area latency constraints. This paper investigates EVPN--VXLAN as an infrastructure foundation for geo-distributed AI training environments and presents a scalable emulation framework for systematically studying distributed AI workloads under realistic wide-area conditions. The proposed framework combines VXLAN overlays with EVPN-based inter-data-center connectivity and is implemented using ContainerLab and FRRouting (FRR). The framework further incorporates Equal-Cost Multi-Path (ECMP) routing, Bidirectional Forwarding Detection (BFD), and a queue-pair-aware traffic distribution mechanism designed to improve communication behavior for synchronization-intensive AI workloads while preserving compatibility with commodity infrastructure. Using realistic WAN emulation, we characterize communication and system behavior under distributed training workloads employing AllReduce and Parameter Server communication patterns. Results provide insights into traffic distribution, resilience, and infrastructure behavior in geo-distributed AI environments, highlighting the potential of reproducible multi-data-center infrastructure frameworks for scalable distributed AI training.

</details>


### [127] [Modular Multi-Domain Digital Twin Architecture: Sustainable Intent-Driven 6G Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13069)
*Berk Buzcu,Marcin Pakula,Gevher Yesevi Keskin,Laura Finarelli,Gianluca Rizzo,Engin Zeydan,Jorge Baranda,Aitor Alcazar-Fernandez,Javier Velazquez-Martinez,Luis M. Contreras,Gil Kedar,Efi Dvir,Paweł Kryszkiewicz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生的6G多域编排架构，通过将网络数字孪生(NDT)作为专用服务域暴露，实现了跨域预测性分析和决策支持，在绿色网络用例中验证了该方法可降低28.5%的电网消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来6G网络将跨越分布式异构域基础设施运行，传统的单域管理已无法满足主动式、可信赖的自动化需求。虽然网络数字孪生(NDT)能够实现假设分析、AI辅助优化和控制行动的风险验证，但单体式端到端孪生在可扩展性、保真度和跨域协调方面面临挑战，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出了一种支持数字孪生的6G架构，将NDT能力作为专用服务域暴露在基于服务的6G多域编排框架中。引入DT编排器解释预测性和规范性假设查询，按需组合特定域的DT模块和仿真器，同时保持请求实体的决策权限。设计了涵盖遥测同步、基于仿真的决策支持和闭环执行的通用工作流程。

Result: 通过一个绿色网络用例验证了框架可行性，该用例将系统级O-RAN蜂窝数字孪生组件与两阶段太阳能分配仿真器耦合，在波兹南105个基站部署上使用仿真数据集进行评估。联合覆盖和可再生能源优化在32块太阳能板的收益递减阈值处，使每日电网消耗降低28.5%，识别出17个基站既是覆盖活跃站点又是高优先级太阳能候选站点。

Conclusion: 跨域NDT协调能够实现可持续、意图驱动的6G网络管理，所提出的架构成功解决了单体式数字孪生的可扩展性和跨域协调问题，为未来6G网络的主动式自动化管理提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Future 6G networks will operate across distributed and heterogeneous domain infrastructures, making conventional single-domain management insufficient for proactive, trustworthy automation. Network Digital Twins (NDTs) enable what-if analysis, AI-assisted optimization, and risk-free validation of control actions before deployment, yet monolithic end-to-end twins remain impractical due to scalability, fidelity, and cross-domain coordination challenges. Accordingly, this paper proposes a Digital Twin-enabled 6G architecture that exposes NDT capabilities as a specialized service domain within a multi-domain orchestration framework built on a state-of-the-art service-based 6G architecture. A DT Orchestrator interprets \textit{predictive} and \textit{prescriptive} what-if queries and composes domain-specific DT modules and simulators on demand, while decision authority remains with the requesting entity. Furthermore, a generalized workflow covers telemetry synchronization, simulation-based decision support, and closed-loop execution. The framework is demonstrated through a green-networking use case that couples a system-level O-RAN cellular digital twin component with a two-stage solar-allocation simulator, evaluated over a 105-base-station deployment in Poznan using simulative datasets. Joint coverage and renewable optimization reduces daily grid consumption by 28.5\% with 32 solar panels at the diminishing-returns threshold, with 17 base stations identified as both coverage-active and high-priority solar candidates as evidence that cross-domain NDT coordination enables sustainable, intent-driven 6G network management.

</details>


### [128] [Revolutionizing Wireless Communications with Space Data Centers: Applications and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13086)
*Minghao Sun,Zehui Chen,Jinbo Hou,Kezhi Wang,Xiaoli Chu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间数据中心（SDCs）如何将空间通信范式从以连接为导向转变为以任务和服务为中心，提出了分层网络架构并通过仿真验证了其在降低控制层延迟方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着未来AI产业的发展，传统卫星作为中继节点或轻量级处理器的功能已无法满足数据密集型和智能驱动的空间应用需求。空间数据中心（SDCs）作为一种新兴的轨道计算基础设施，能够集成通信、计算、存储和控制能力，为空间应用提供持续服务支持。

Method: 提出了包含接入层、中继层、计算层和控制层的分层SDC网络架构，并概述了可能的部署策略。探讨了SDCs支持的代表性未来应用场景，分析了其通信特征和面临的研究挑战，并通过仿真实验验证架构性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SDCs在分层空间网络中有效降低了控制层延迟，验证了该架构的有效性。同时识别了SDCs实际部署面临的关键研究方向和挑战。

Conclusion: 空间数据中心将推动空间通信范式从以连接为导向的数据传输向以任务为导向、以服务为中心的信息交换转变。文章为SDCs的实际部署指明了关键研究方向，对构建未来空间AI基础设施具有重要意义。

Abstract: Space data centers (SDCs) are emerging as a promising orbital computing infrastructure for the future AI industry. Unlike conventional satellites that mainly serve as relay nodes or lightweight onboard processors, SDCs integrate communication, computing, storage, and control capabilities in orbit, enabling persistent service support for data-intensive and intelligence-driven space applications. In this article, we investigate how SDCs may transform space communication paradigms from connectivity-oriented data transmission toward task-oriented and service-centric information exchange. We first present a hierarchical SDC network architecture consisting of access, relay, computing, and control layers, and outline possible deployment strategies. We then explore representative future application scenarios enabled by SDCs, highlighting their communication characteristics and associated research challenges. Simulation results further demonstrate the effectiveness of SDCs in reducing control-layer latency in hierarchical space networks. Finally, we identify key research directions toward the practical deployment of SDCs.

</details>


### [129] [Feasibility Assessment of Remote Driving via Latency Analysis of ITS-G5 and Cellular Networks in the MASA Living Lab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13292)
*Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Antonio Solida,Salvatore Iandolo,Marco Savarese,Martin Klapez,Enrico Rossini,Marcello Pietri,Marco Picone,Marco Mamei,Maurizio Casoni,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在城市级真实测试台中对比分析了ITS-G5和5G网络的延迟性能，评估了远程驾驶的可行性，并提出了混合通信策略建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶作为网联自动驾驶的关键使能技术受到广泛关注，但其实际部署依赖于无线网络能够保证低且可预测的延迟。当前缺乏在真实城市环境中对ITS-G5和5G技术的全面延迟分析，阻碍了远程驾驶的安全部署。

Method: 在Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA)城市级测试台进行受控实验，测量不同网络负载和交通条件下的网络延迟和端到端延迟。延迟测量考虑了远程驾驶场景：上行链路传输连续视频流（车辆到远程操作员），下行链路传输控制指令（返回车辆）。对比分析ITS-G5和蜂窝网络（5G）技术的性能差异，并评估基础设施覆盖范围对视频流传输的影响。

Result: 测量结果揭示了两种技术在延迟和变异性方面的差异，以及基础设施覆盖范围如何影响视频流传输性能。基于观察到的延迟分布和可靠性指标，评估了混合网络环境中远程驾驶的可行性和安全裕度。分析结果表明ITS-G5和5G各具优势，单一技术难以完全满足远程驾驶的需求。

Conclusion: 研究结果为未来远程操作部署提供了可操作的见解，并提出了结合ITS-G5和蜂窝网络优势的混合通信策略，以提高远程驾驶的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Remote driving has gained increasing attention as a key enabler for connected and automated vehicles. Yet its practical deployment hinges on wireless networks' ability to guarantee low, predictable latency. In this paper, we present an extensive latency analysis of ITS-G5 and cellular (5G) technologies within the Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA), a real-world, city-scale testbed equipped with a distributed intelligent transportation infrastructure. By conducting controlled experiments under varying network loads and traffic conditions, we measure network and end-to-end latency components relevant to remote driving, in which the uplink consists of a continuous video stream transmitted from the vehicle to the remote operator, and the downlink conveys control commands back to the car. Measurements conducted under diverse conditions reveal how latency and variability differ across the two technologies and how infrastructure coverage impacts video-stream transmission performance. Based on the observed latency distributions and reliability metrics, we assess the practical feasibility and safety margins of remote driving in mixed network environments. The results provide actionable insights for future teleoperation deployments and motivate hybrid communication strategies that combine the strengths of ITS-G5 and cellular networks.

</details>


### [130] [Measurement-Based Performance Evaluation of SmartRSUs with Heterogeneous Antenna Architectures for V2X Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13334)
*Marco Savarese,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Salvatore Iandolo,Antonio Solida Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Micaela Verucchi,Enrico Vincenzi,Ignacio Sanudo Olmedo,Marko Bertogna,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对两种采用不同V2X天线架构的定制智能路侧单元(SmartRSU)进行了实测性能评估，结果表明天线设计与部署位置对通信可靠性和延迟存在显著影响，揭示了集成天线与外置天线配置在真实部署场景中的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对下一代协作智能交通系统(C-ITS)中智能路侧单元的设计优化需求，研究不同V2X天线架构（集成式与外置式）在实际部署环境下的性能差异，为SmartRSU的设计提供实践指导和客观验证。

Method: 构建两种定制SmartRSU原型：第一种采用GNSS与通信天线集成的一体化车顶模块，第二种使用外置双ITS-G5(IEEE 802.11p)天线(5.9 GHz)与专用GNSS天线，均基于适配基础设施部署的专有车载单元(OBU)平台。在同一基础设施上同址部署商用现货V2X路侧单元作为性能基准，在相同环境条件下开展受控实验。评估关键V2X通信指标：覆盖范围、接收信号强度指示(RSSI)、丢包率、端到端延迟（包括OBU到基础设施的发送方向和基础设施到OBU的接收方向）。

Result: 实验结果揭示了天线设计和部署位置对通信可靠性和延迟的显著影响，明确了集成天线与外置天线配置在真实部署场景中存在的性能权衡。相比商用基准设备，两种定制方案在不同指标上表现出各自优势与局限。

Conclusion: 研究为下一代SmartRSU的设计与优化提供了重要的实践洞察，证明了天线架构选择需根据具体部署场景的可靠性与延迟需求进行权衡决策，为C-ITS基础设施的工程部署提供了客观的参考依据。

Abstract: This paper presents a measurement-based performance evaluation of two custom Smart Roadside Units (SmartRSUs) featuring different V2X antenna architectures. The first configuration integrates GNSS and communication antennas into an all-in-one rooftop module, whereas the second uses external dual ITS-G5 (IEEE 802.11p) antennas operating at 5.9~GHz and a dedicated GNSS antenna. Both systems are built upon a proprietary On-Board Unit (OBU) platform adapted for infrastructure deployment.
  The experimental campaign evaluates key V2X communication metrics, including coverage, received signal strength indicator (RSSI), packet loss, and end-to-end latency in both transmission (OBU-to-infrastructure) and reception (infrastructure-to-OBU) directions. To ensure objective validation, a commercial off-the-shelf V2X Roadside Unit is co-located on the same infrastructure and used as a performance benchmark, providing ground-truth reference measurements under identical environmental conditions through a controlled co-located deployment.
  Results highlight the impact of antenna design and placement on communication reliability and latency, revealing trade-offs between integrated and external antenna configurations in real-world deployment scenarios. The findings provide practical insights for the design and optimization of next-generation SmartRSUs in cooperative intelligent transportation systems (C-ITS).

</details>


### [131] [Experimental Insights into UDP-Based Video and Control Traffic over IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13390)
*Antonio Solida,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Salvatore Iandolo,Marco Savarese,Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验评估了IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5测试平台上UDP传输的性能，发现调制方案是低负载下影响延迟的主导因素，而传输模式和IP版本在拥塞条件下变得重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载应用（如协同驾驶、远程操作和实时感知）日益依赖低延迟无线通信，ITS-G5是实现车对车和车对基础设施直接通信的关键技术。然而，在现实条件下针对IEEE 802.11p上UDP流量性能的实验研究仍然有限，缺乏实际配置指导。

Method: 在由树莓派车载单元和商用路侧单元组成的IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5测试床上进行实验评估。研究了不同调制编码方案(MCS)的影响，评估了两种网络层配置（IPv4单播和IPv6多播）以及CAKE主动队列管理的使用。除使用iPerf生成合成流量外，还包括通过MPEG-TS over UDP进行实时视频流传输，以模拟延迟敏感的车载应用。

Result: 调制方案是低流量负载下影响延迟的主导因素，而传输模式和IP版本的选择在拥塞条件下变得日益重要。高阶调制显著降低了延迟和变异性，而IPv6多播比IPv4单播表现出更大的延迟分散。此外，主动队列管理似乎并不能改善延迟的可预测性。

Conclusion: 这些发现为配置支持延迟敏感车载服务的ITS-G5网络提供了实用的见解和建议，有助于优化车载通信系统的性能配置。

Abstract: Vehicular applications such as cooperative driving, teleoperation, and real-time perception increasingly rely on low-latency wireless communication. In this context, ITS-G5, based on IEEE 802.11p, represents a key technology for enabling direct vehicle-to-vehicle and vehicle-to-infrastructure communication. Despite its relevance, experimental studies focusing on the performance of UDP-based traffic over IEEE 802.11p under realistic conditions remain limited.
  This paper presents an experimental evaluation of UDP transmission over an IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5 testbed composed of Raspberry Pi-based onboard units and commercial roadside units. The analysis investigates the impact of different modulation and coding schemes (MCS). It also evaluates two network-layer configurations (IPv4 unicast and IPv6 multicast) and the use of CAKE for active queue management. In addition to synthetic traffic generated with iPerf, the evaluation includes real-time video streaming using MPEG-TS over UDP to emulate latency-sensitive vehicular applications. Results show that the modulation scheme is the dominant factor influencing latency at low traffic loads, while the choice of transmission mode and IP version becomes increasingly significant under congested conditions. Higher-order modulations significantly reduce latency and variability, whereas IPv6 multicast exhibits greater delay dispersion than IPv4 unicast. Furthermore, active queue management does not seem to improve delay predictability. These findings provide practical insights for configuring ITS-G5 networks supporting latency-sensitive vehicular services.

</details>


### [132] [From Traditional Automation to Embodied Wireless Intelligence: Vision-Language-Action Empowered Physics-Aware Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13458)
*Genze Jiang,Kezhi Wang,Xiaomin Chen,Yizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了具身智能赋能基站（eBS）范式，通过视觉-语言-动作（VLA）管道将基站转变为能够进行情境感知、因果物理推理和物理感知动作生成的自主智能体，实现了从传统基于规则的网络自动化到具身智能的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线网络自动化系统虽然已从基于规则的自组织网络（SON）发展到数据驱动优化，但本质上仍是非具身的，仅优化性能指标而无法感知支配无线电传播的物理环境，这限制了网络优化的效果和智能化程度。

Method: 提出具身智能赋能基站（eBS）架构，采用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）管道，包含两层异步架构：（1）语义规划器：由前沿视觉-语言模型（VLM）驱动，在人类时间尺度上生成结构化动作指令；（2）战术控制器：执行实时自适应控制。该架构使基站具备情境感知、因果物理推理和物理感知动作生成能力。

Result: 案例研究表明，单个VLA管道无需任务特定训练即可实现：零样本材质推理、跨视角泛化能力、在信号降质发生前预测动态事件，展示了从传统规则遵循型网络自动化到具身智能赋能的未来无线网络的范式转变。

Conclusion: eBS范式标志着无线网络自动化从传统基于规则的方法向具身智能的重要转变，为未来无线网络提供了具备环境感知和物理推理能力的智能化解决方案，开辟了无线网络自动化的新方向。

Abstract: Wireless network automation has progressed from rule-based self-organising networks (SON) to data-driven optimisation, yet existing systems remain fundamentally disembodied, optimising performance indicators without perceiving the physical environment that governs radio propagation. We propose the embodied intelligent empowered base station (eBS), a paradigm that adopts a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) pipeline to transform base stations into autonomous agents capable of situated perception, causal physical reasoning, and physics-aware action generation. The eBS employs a two-tier asynchronous architecture: a Semantic Planner powered by a frontier Vision-Language Model (VLM) generates structured action directives on human timescales, whilst a Tactical Controller executes real-time adaptation. Case studies demonstrate that a single VLA pipeline, without task-specific training, can perform zero-shot material reasoning, generalise across viewpoints, and predict dynamic events before signal degradation occurs, illustrating a paradigm shift from traditional rule-following network automation to embodied intelligence empowered future wireless networks.

</details>


### [133] [Graphical Causal Reasoning for Root Cause Analysis in Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13532)
*Fabien Chraim,Dominik Janzing,John Evans*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图因果发现技术的云计算网络故障根因分析(RCA)方法，通过时空分组和自动化本体降低问题维度，在35个生产事故数据集上达到85.7%的召回率和74.3%的完全匹配率，并在超过800个实际事件中得到部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算依赖的大规模网络具有内在复杂性，现有的基于规则的自动化根因分析方法存在局限性，难以有效处理动态、大规模运营环境中的网络故障诊断问题。

Method: 提出基于图的因果发现方法：(1)采用时空分组策略和自动化本体降低问题维度；(2)利用二元时间序列数据，通过双变量格兰杰因果检验和条件独立性检验构建因果图；(3)引入概率推理方法，为边分配基于时间滞后条件的特定条件概率，通过因果图遍历实现可解释、时间感知的根因评分。

Result: 在来自主要云服务提供商的35个标注生产事故数据集上验证，模型成功召回85.7%事故的正确根因，74.3%产生精确匹配。在生产环境中，该系统已应用于超过800个真实世界事故，并获得网络工程师的积极定性反馈。

Conclusion: 研究证明了数据驱动的因果方法在动态大规模运营环境中进行根因分析的实用性，为云计算网络故障诊断提供了一种有效、可解释且时间感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud-computing relies on large-scale networks which are inherently complex systems. In this paper, we present a novel approach to root cause analysis (RCA) of cloud network incidents, leveraging graph-based causal discovery techniques. Our method addresses the limitations of rule-based automation by introducing a spatiotemporal grouping strategy and an automation ontology to reduce the dimensionality of the problem. We construct a causal graph from binary time series data using bivariate Granger causality and conditional independence tests. For inference, we introduce a probabilistic method that assigns edge-specific conditional probabilities as a function of time lag, allowing for interpretable, time-aware root cause scoring via causal graph traversal.
  We evaluated the system using a labeled dataset of 35 production incidents from a major cloud provider. The model successfully recalled the correct root cause in 85.7% of incidents and produced an exact match in 74.3%. In production, the deployed system has been used in over 800 real-world incidents, with positive qualitative feedback from network engineers. These results highlight the practicality of a data-driven, causal approach to RCA in dynamic and large-scale operational environments.

</details>


### [134] [NetCause: Counterfactual Learning for Root Cause Analysis in Large-Scale Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13543)
*Fabien Chraim,Jian Zhang,Dominik Janzing,Xiang Song,Christos Faloutsos,John Evans*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NetCause框架，一种基于自监督学习的方法，通过图时序过程建模和反事实仿真来对网络故障的根本原因进行排序，在云服务商的真实数据上相比基于规则的基线方法准确率提升了16.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的根本原因分析技术通常依赖静态规则、相关性启发式方法或局部拓扑推理，在故障通过复杂的物理和逻辑依赖关系传播的动态环境中难以泛化，无法有效捕获故障在大规模网络中的传播机制并因果归因客户影响。

Method: 提出NetCause框架，将网络事故建模为图时序过程，利用自监督学习方法训练模型，并通过反事实仿真对候选根本原因进行排序，生成可解释的根本原因假设排序，并能与运维人员定义的缓解和修复动作自然集成。

Result: 在某领先云服务商生产网络中收集的六个月内超过1,500起事故数据上训练模型，并在31个专家标注的事故上进行评估。NetCause在运维决策最相关的场景中持续提升根本原因排序质量，相比基于规则的启发式基线方法准确率提升16.1%。虽然训练计算密集，但推理轻量级，每个事故仅需数秒GPU运行时间。

Conclusion: NetCause成功证明了学习模型能够捕获故障在大规模网络中的传播机制，并利用该知识因果归因客户影响，为网络故障的根本原因分析提供了一种有效、可解释且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Can a learned model capture how faults propagate through a large-scale network and use this knowledge to causally attribute customer impact to its underlying root cause? Existing root cause analysis techniques often rely on static rules, correlation heuristics, or topology-local reasoning, which struggle to generalize in dynamic environments where faults propagate across complex physical and logical dependencies.
  We present NetCause, a self-supervised learning-based framework that models network incidents as graph-temporal processes and uses counterfactual simulation to rank candidate root causes. This approach produces an interpretable ranking of root cause hypotheses and integrates naturally with operator-defined mitigation and remediation actions.
  We train the model on over 1,500 incidents collected over six months from a leading cloud provider's production network and evaluate it on 31 expert-labeled incidents. NetCause consistently improves root cause ranking quality in the regime most relevant to operational decision-making, achieving a 16.1% accuracy improvement over a rule-based heuristic baseline. While training is computationally intensive, inference is lightweight, requiring only seconds of GPU runtime per incident (well below typical telemetry collection latencies).

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [SAIGuard: Communication-State Simulation for Proactive Defense of LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12474)
*Ruxue Shi,Yili Wang,Mengnan Du,Qinggang Zhang,Rui Miao,Yixin Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAIGuard，一种针对大语言模型多智能体系统的主动防御框架，通过通信状态模拟和消息净化/重生成，在恶意消息传播前进行拦截，相比现有反应式防御方法，能更有效地降低攻击成功率并保持系统协作效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）防御主要采用反应式范式（检测并隔离有害智能体），但这种方式可能导致不可逆的损害并降低协作效用。MAS的通信驱动特性使得安全风险能够在智能体间传播并引发系统性故障，因此需要一种主动防御机制。

Method: 提出SAIGuard主动防御框架：1）在MAS交互图上执行通信状态模拟；2）估计传入消息对局部智能体状态和全局MAS状态的影响；3）通过与良性通信模式的重建偏差来检测危险消息；4）不隔离智能体，而是在可疑消息传播到系统之前对其进行净化或重生成。

Result: 在多种拓扑结构和攻击场景下的实验表明，SAIGuard能够有效降低攻击成功率，同时保持MAS的协作效用，整体性能优于反应式防御方法。

Conclusion: SAIGuard为MAS安全提供了一种有效的主动防御方案，通过消息拦截与净化机制，在保障系统安全的同时维护了协作效用，克服了传统反应式防御的局限性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) solve complex tasks through inter-agent collaboration, but their communication-driven nature also allows security risks to spread across agents and trigger system-wide failures. Existing MAS defenses mainly follow a reactive paradigm after execution by detecting and isolating harmful agents, which may cause irreversible damage and degrade collaborative utility. To address this, we propose a proactive defense framework for MAS security, namely a Simulation-aware Interception Guard (SAIGuard). SAIGuard performs communication-state simulation over the MAS interaction graph, estimates the impact of incoming messages on local agent states and the global MAS state, and detects risky messages via reconstruction deviations from benign communication patterns. Instead of isolating agents, SAIGuard sanitizes or regenerates suspicious messages before it propagation into system. Experiments across diverse topologies and attack scenarios show that SAIGuard reduces attack success rates while maintaining MAS utility, outperforming reactive defenses.

</details>


### [136] [Smarter Saboteurs, Better Fixers: Scaling & Security in Linear Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12709)
*Timothy McAllister,Sina Abdidizaji,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Ozmen Garibay*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 大规模模型更容易被恶意指令利用，但在线性工作流末端添加修复器可恢复安全性，证明线性结构在合适防护下具备韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模型规模对多智能体系统安全性的影响，特别是线性协作结构如何受到模型scaling与对抗攻击的影响。

Method: 在HumanEval基准上对两个开源模型家族的不同规模进行实验，分析恶意指令注入与修复机制对线性工作流安全性的作用。

Result: 较大模型忠实执行恶意指令的概率显著增加（27B模型性能下降53.7pp），但增加轻量修复器后可将下降减少至0.6pp并恢复控制水平。

Conclusion: 线性协作结构在具备修复机制时具有安全性，之前归因于线性拓扑的脆弱性可能源于缺乏纠错机制。

Abstract: As LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are deployed in the wild, the resilience of their collaboration structures against adversarial compromise becomes a critical safety concern. Attackers may leverage prompt-injection or jailbreaking to sabotage individual agents within MAS workflows, but the interaction between model scaling and system-level resilience remains poorly understood. This paper investigates how model scale affects the security of linear multi-agent workflows. Our experiments across scales of two open-weight model families on the HumanEval benchmark reveal a compliance-correction symmetry: larger models are far more likely to faithfully execute malicious instructions, with the control-to-malicious performance drop reaching 53.7pp at 27B in uncorrected pipelines. However, appending a lightweight terminal Fixer stage collapses this to 0.6pp and restores statistical parity with control-level performance, demonstrating that strictly linear collaboration structures can be viable and resilient to adversaries at this scale, and suggesting that the brittleness previously attributed to linear topology may stem from a lack of correction.

</details>


### [137] [The Internet of Agentic AI: Communication, Coordination, and Collective Intelligence at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12835)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了智联网愿景，旨在构建一个开放的异构智能体生态系统，使其能够在云、边、端及组织间环境中自主发现、协商、协作并执行工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体的快速发展，AI正从孤立模型推理转变为分布式推理、通信与行动系统，亟需建立统一框架以支撑大规模异构智能体间的协作与互操作。

Method: 综合单智能体AI、多智能体系统、分布式计算、通信网络、博弈论与安全工程等理论基础，系统分析智能体部署模型、工作流生命周期、通信协议、互操作性层、资源管理与信任架构，并结合自适应制造等案例进行验证。

Result: 提出了智联网架构框架，明确了实现可扩展智能体生态系统的核心机制，包括智能体发现、责任协商、上下文交换、工具调用与跨域工作流执行等关键能力。

Conclusion: 识别并阐释了大规模自主智能体网络面临的核心研究挑战：受控涌现、语义互操作性、安全身份、激励兼容协调、资源感知编排与治理，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of autonomous AI agents is transforming artificial intelligence from isolated model inference into distributed systems of reasoning, communication, and action. This paper develops the vision of the Internet of Agentic AI (IoAI): an open ecosystem in which heterogeneous agents discover one another, negotiate responsibilities, exchange context, invoke tools, and execute workflows across cloud, edge, device, organizational, and cyber-physical environments. We synthesize foundations from single-agent agentic AI, multi-agent systems, distributed computing, communication networks, game theory, and security engineering to characterize the architectures and mechanisms required for scalable agent ecosystems. The paper examines agent deployment models, workflow lifecycles, communication protocols, interoperability layers, resource-management challenges, and trust architectures, with case studies in adaptive manufacturing and distributed operational coordination. The resulting framework highlights the central research challenges of controlled emergence, semantic interoperability, secure identity, incentive-compatible coordination, resource-aware orchestration, and governance for large-scale networks of autonomous agents.

</details>


### [138] [Effects of Social Interactions in Self-Organising Railway Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13068)
*Fabio Oddi,Federico Naldini,Leo D'Amato,Grégory Marlière,Paola Pellegrini,Vito Trianni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在自组织交通管理系统中，研究发现短预测邻域时域即可满足需求，长时域反而降低局部可处理性和计算响应性，且无全局调度优化增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自组织交通管理系统中，列车需要预测邻域、生成交通计划假设并通过共识机制与邻居协商未来交通计划。预测邻域时域作为该流程中的关键结构参数，用于识别未来潜在冲突并确定局部交互拓扑，其选择直接影响社交交互图的大小和密度，同时涉及局部子问题复杂性与分布式共识动态之间的权衡。

Method: 通过闭环仿真框架评估不同时域取值对去中心化协调过程的影响，覆盖从初始冲突检测到分布式调度共识的全流程。重点分析时域选择引入的权衡：在安全关键环境中平衡局部可处理性和计算响应性与全局调度一致性和可行性需求。

Result: 实证结果与直觉相反：短时域已足够满足系统需求，而长时域不仅损害局部可处理性和计算响应性，还未能带来全局调度最优性的提升。

Conclusion: 在自组织交通管理系统的设计中，应采用较短的预测邻域时域。这一发现为去中心化交通协调系统的参数设计提供了重要指导，表明在保证全局调度性能的前提下，可以通过缩短时域来优化系统的计算效率和局部处理能力。

Abstract: Recent research is exploring self-organised traffic management as a solution for scaling to complex real-world networks. In such a system, trains predict their neighbourhood, produce traffic plan hypotheses, and agree via consensus with neighbours on a future traffic plan to be implemented. This paper investigates a structural parameter within this pipeline: the predictive neighbourhood horizon. The horizon is used by trains to identify future potential conflicts with neighbours, and to establish the local interaction topology, that is, the subset of trains to negotiate with. As the primary design variable, the horizon directly determines the size and density of the social interaction graph, whereas its impact on the complexity of local sub-problems and the distributed consensus dynamics represents a trade-off to be explored. Through a closed-loop simulation framework the study evaluates how variations of the horizon impact the overall decentralised coordination process, from initial conflict detection to distributed schedule consensus. The analysis focuses on investigating the potential trade-off introduced by the horizon choice: balancing local tractability and computational responsiveness with the need for global schedule coherence and feasibility in safety-critical environments. Contrary to intuition, our empirical results indicate that the short time horizons suffice, while long values compromise local tractability and computational responsiveness with no gain in global schedule optimality.

</details>


### [139] [$α$-fair heterogeneous agent reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13076)
*Yao-hua Franck Xu,Tayeb Lemlouma,Jean-Marie Bonnin,Arnaud Braud*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将α-公平性与异构智能体信赖域学习相结合的新框架，在保证理论收敛性的同时实现了更公平且更高效的多智能体协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体协作优化通常采用功利主义目标，虽然能最大化整体效率，但忽视了奖励分配的公平性，常导致不公平的"领导者-追随者"动态。虽然基于公平性的方法能鼓励亲社会行为，但现有算法（包括使用奖励塑形的方法）要么破坏马尔可夫博弈的平稳性，要么缺乏严格的理论保证，在公平目标方法与理论安全学习框架之间存在关键差距。

Method: 提出一个将α-公平性与异构智能体信赖域学习（HATRL）相结合的新框架，确保单调改进并收敛到纳什均衡。该方法利用公平优势函数，根据智能体的预期回报动态加权其效用，使全局目标能够根据参数α从纯功利主义效率过渡到α-公平性福利。基于此框架提出了两个实用算法：α-fair HATRPO和α-fair HAPPO。

Result: 在CleanUp和CommonHarvest等序贯社会困境实验中，所提出的算法从功利主义角度看比HATRL的算法表现更好，同时实现了更高的社会结果。

Conclusion: 该研究成功弥合了公平目标方法与理论安全学习框架之间的差距，证明了在多智能体系统中引入公平性考虑不仅不会损害效率，反而能在保持理论保证的同时实现更好的整体性能和社会结果。

Abstract: Cooperation in multi-agent systems is typically optimized through utilitarian objectives that maximize overall efficiency but fail to account for reward distribution, often resulting in inequitable "leader-follower" dynamics. While fairness-based approaches encourage pro-social behaviors where every agent benefits from cooperation, many current algorithms - including those utilizing reward shaping - break the stationarity of Markov Games or lack rigorous theoretical guarantees. This creates a critical gap between fair objective methods and theoretically safe learning frameworks. We propose a novel framework that bridges $α$-fairness with Heterogeneous-Agent Trust Region Learning (HATRL), ensuring monotonic improvement and convergence toward Nash Equilibria. Our approach leverages a fair advantage function that dynamically weights agent utilities based on their expected returns, allowing the global objective to transition from purely utilitarian efficiency to $α$-fairness welfare based on the parameter $α$. We introduce two practical algorithms, $α$-fair HATRPO and $α$-fair HAPPO, and demonstrate through experiments in sequential social dilemmas like CleanUp and CommonHarvest that they perform better than HATRL's algorithms from a utilitarian point of view while achieving socially higher outcomes.

</details>


### [140] [See What I See, Know What I Think: Dense Latent Communication Across Heterogeneous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13594)
*Siyi Chen,Xiaoyan Zhang,Meng Wu,Jonathan Tremblay,Valts Blukis,Stan Birchfield,Rene Vidal,Alvaro Velasquez,Sijia Liu,Qing Qu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种密集对齐方法，通过轻量级跨模型缓存变换和两阶段训练，实现了异构智能体间高效的KV缓存通信。在六种模型方向和六个基准测试中，该方法优于异构基线，计算成本比文本通信低2-3倍，并在无上下文场景中保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统主要通过文本通信，产生有损且昂贵的解码和重新编码成本。KV缓存通信是一种有前景的替代方案，但现有工作主要局限于同构模型，异构方法也存在诸多限制。本文研究了一个根本性问题：异构智能体能否实现真正的"心智读取"，既传递一个智能体所见内容，又传递其思维方式？

Method: 通过信息结构分析揭示了上下文感知与无感知转移的对偶性，提出了针对异构KV缓存通信的密集对齐方法。方法包含轻量级跨模型缓存变换和两阶段训练流程：首先是重建阶段，然后是生成阶段。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B、8B、14B三种模型的六个方向和六个领域内外基准测试中，该方法优于现有异构基线。在上下文感知设置中，性能匹配或超越文本通信，计算成本降低约2-3倍。在先前方法失效的上下文无感知转移场景中，该方法依然有效。

Conclusion: 通过密集对齐方法，异构智能体之间可以实现有效的KV缓存通信，避免了文本通信的高成本，扩展了异构智能体协作的能力边界，为多智能体系统提供了更高效的通信范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems communicate mostly through text, paying a lossy and expensive decode and re-encode cost. KV-cache communication is a promising alternative, yet most prior work is homogeneous, using duplicate copies of the same model, and avoids the central challenge of cross-model latent alignment; existing heterogeneous methods are also restrictive, typically assuming shared input and using transferred caches mainly for steering. We study a more fundamental question: can heterogeneous agents be aligned well enough to perform real "mind reading" and transfer both what one agent sees and how it thinks? Our information-structure analysis reveals a duality: context-aware transfer is driven by sparse reasoning signals, while context-unaware transfer, where the receiver sees no input, requires dense contextual knowledge preservation. Motivated by this, we propose dense alignment for heterogeneous KV-cache communication via a lightweight cross-model cache transformation and two-phase training: reconstruction followed by generation. Across all six directions of {Qwen3-4B, 8B, 14B} and six in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, our method outperforms prior heterogeneous baselines, matches or exceeds text communication in context-aware settings at roughly 2 to 3 times lower compute, and remains effective in context-unaware transfer where prior methods collapse.

</details>


### [141] [Tuning Agent-Based Predator-Prey Models Toward Lotka-Volterra Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13639)
*Corinna Mandl,Siddharth Chaturvedi,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于神经网络的捕食者-猎物智能体模型，通过优化环境和人口参数，成功复现了类似经典Lotka-Volterra模型的种群周期性振荡动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基于智能体的模型在模拟复杂适应系统时，面临参数敏感性问题：局部规则和参数的微小变化可能导致系统行为失控、种群崩溃或饱和。如何在包含神经网络控制器的连续捕食者-猎物系统中调节参数以产生稳定的种群振荡是一个核心挑战。

Method: 构建了一个连续的捕食者-猎物系统，其中羊和狼作为具有局部感知、内部能量和循环神经网络控制器的智能体。采用基于特征的损失函数来优化环境和人口参数，该损失函数奖励持续的振荡、相位滞后、有界种群和长期持续性。优化过程分为两阶段：首先针对随机控制器，然后针对进化控制器。模型使用基于JAX的智能体建模框架ABMax实现，支持在硬件加速器上进行高效的批量模拟。

Result: 通过参数优化，成功使基于智能体的系统产生了类似经典Lotka-Volterra循环的种群动态，实现了持续的种群振荡和长期持续性。ABMax框架证明了其在处理大规模智能体模型时的计算效率优势。

Conclusion: 尽管智能体模型具有高复杂性和参数敏感性，但通过系统化的参数优化方法，可以诱导产生期望的宏观种群动态行为，这为复杂适应系统的可控模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent growth in compute power has made it increasingly feasible to use large-scale agent-based models to simulate complex adaptive systems. A central difficulty is that such models contain many local rules and parameters, where small changes can lead to runaway behaviour, population collapse, or saturation at artificial bounds. We study this problem in a continuous predator-prey system where sheep and wolves are active agents with local sensing, internal energy, and recurrent neural network-based controllers. We ask whether environmental and demographic parameters can be tuned so that the resulting population dynamics resemble classical Lotka-Volterra cycles. We optimise these parameters with a feature-based loss that rewards sustained oscillations, phase lag, bounded populations, and long-term persistence, first for random controllers and then for evolved controllers in a more naturalistic setting. The model is implemented in ABMax, a JAX-based agent-based modelling framework that enables efficient batched simulation on hardware accelerators.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Eidola: Modeling Multi-GPU Network Communication Traffic in Distributed AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12638)
*Ranganath R. Selagamsetty,Matthew Poremba,Bradford M. Beckmann,Joshua San Miguel,Mikko H. Lipasti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Eidola，一个基于gem5的可扩展仿真框架扩展，用于精确建模多GPU系统中的GPU间通信流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式AI工作负载规模的增长，多GPU系统对于训练大模型至关重要。然而，内核融合和通信计算重叠等技术引入了不规则且瞬态的流量模式，现有的工具难以对其进行建模。这些技术严重依赖细粒度同步和点对点通信，给互连带宽和延迟带来了巨大压力。

Method: 开发了一个可扩展的gem5框架扩展——Eidola。该扩展采用简洁的GPU模型作为“eidolon”（幻影），仅模拟流量建模所需的最小特征。Eidola利用来自真实应用程序的带注释时序配置文件，以周期级精度模拟点对点GPU写入，并支持可配置的每GPU流量模式以及隔离的性能分析。

Result: 通过复现融合内核执行的可变性以及实现SyncMon启发的同步机制，验证了Eidola的有效性。实验结果确认了轮询相关内存流量的减少，表明该工具能够准确模拟同步行为。

Conclusion: Eidola为研究GPU间通信提供了一个灵活且可扩展的平台，能够支持现代分布式GPU系统中的架构探索，解决了现有工具难以建模不规则通信流量模式的难题。

Abstract: As distributed AI workloads grow in scale, multi-GPU systems have become essential for training large models. Although techniques like kernel fusion and overlapping communication with computation help reduce delays, they also introduce irregular and transient traffic patterns that are difficult to model using existing tools. These techniques rely heavily on fine-grained synchronization and peer-to-peer communication, which place significant pressure on interconnect bandwidth and latency.
  In this work, we introduce Eidola, a scalable extension to the gem5 simulation framework that enables detailed modeling of inter-GPU communication traffic. The extension is scalable as our GPU model serves as a succinct eidolon, emulating the minimal characteristics needed for traffic modeling. Eidola uses annotated timing profiles from real applications to emulate peer-to-peer GPU writes with cycle-level precision. This allows researchers to simulate and analyze synchronization behavior across large multi-GPU configurations. The simulator supports configurable per-GPU traffic patterns and enables isolated performance analysis under different communication scenarios.
  We demonstrate Eidola's effectiveness by reproducing variability in fused kernel execution and by implementing a SyncMon-inspired synchronization mechanism, confirming reductions in polling-related memory traffic. Our results show that Eidola provides a flexible and scalable platform for studying inter-GPU communication and supports architectural exploration in modern distributed GPU systems.

</details>


### [143] [ITME: Inference Tiered Memory Expansion with Disaggregated CXL-Hybrid Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12556)
*Hakbeom Jang,Younghoon Min,Sunwoong Kim,Taeyoung Ahn,Hanyee Kim,Youngpyo Joo,Hoshik Kim,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ITME，利用CXL混合内存实现TB级字节寻址远程内存扩展，通过确定性访问模式主动管理数据移动，在共享上下文基础设施中实现35.7%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型向智能体和长上下文工作负载的快速转变推动行业超越单服务器容量限制，转向分布式共享存储架构处理TB级上下文状态。尽管基于JBOF架构的DPU卸载可加速NVMe-oF目标处理，但复杂的软件级优化和成本效率负担仍然显著，共享上下文基础设施的理想扩展架构仍在探索中。

Method: 提出ITME（推理分层内存扩展）架构，利用CXL混合内存提供大规模TB级字节寻址远程内存扩展。核心洞察是模型权重和前缀缓存的海量确定性访问模式使系统能够主动管理内存-存储层次结构中的数据移动。通过直接字节寻址简化软件栈，使用生产级SK海力士CMM和PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSD进行性能评估，并通过FPGA硬件原型验证功能可行性。

Result: ITME通过提供额外远程内存扩展增强传统CPU卸载能力，能够容纳超出主机内存限制的大型KV缓存足迹，实现高达35.7%的吞吐量提升，同时实现成本高效的扩展并简化软件栈。

Conclusion: ITME成功解决了共享上下文基础设施的关键挑战，通过CXL混合内存架构实现了成本高效的大规模内存扩展和软件栈简化，为大语言模型的智能体和长上下文工作负载提供了可行的基础设施解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid shift toward agentic and long-context workloads in Large Language Models (LLMs) is pushing the industry beyond the capacity of individual servers toward disaggregated shared storage to handle TB-scale context states. This movement has led to the emergence of specialized shared context layers designed to externalize and share cumulative inference states across distributed clusters. While offloading to a data processing unit (DPU) within just-a-bunch-of-flash (JBOF) architectures accelerates NVMe-over-fabrics (NVMe-oF) target processing, the need for sophisticated software-level optimization and cost-efficiency burdens remain significant. Consequently, the ideal architecture for scaling this shared context infrastructure is still an active area of exploration. In this paper, we propose ITME (Inference Tiered Memory Expansion), which leverages a CXL-hybrid memory to present a massive, TB-scale byte-addressable remote memory expansion. This approach enables cost-efficient scaling and simplifies the software stack through direct byte-addressability, effectively addressing the challenges of shared context infrastructure. Our key insight is that the deterministic access patterns of voluminous model weights and prefix caches enable the system to proactively manage data movement across the memory-storage hierarchy. We validate ITME by evaluating its performance potential with production-grade SK Hynix CMM and PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSDs, while further demonstrating its functional feasibility through an FPGA-based hardware prototype. Overall, ITME enhances conventional CPU-offloading by providing additional remote memory expansion to accommodate large KV cache footprints beyond host memory limits, achieving up to a 35.7\% throughput improvement.

</details>


### [144] [On the Limits of Performance Portability in Directive-Based GPU Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12753)
*Alessandro Romeo,Nitin Shukla,Stefano Truzzi,Alessio Suriano,Andrea Mignone*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究通过将天体物理磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC移植到OpenMP，评估了GPU编程的性能可移植性。发现在NVIDIA A100上两者性能相当，但在AMD MI250X上应用级性能下降约3倍，内核级最高下降47倍，主要受内存访问模式和编译器限制影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用向GPU加速的百亿亿次系统过渡受到性能、可移植性和生产力之间权衡的限制。需要评估基于指令的GPU编程在异构架构间的性能可移植性，理解不同GPU平台上的性能瓶颈。

Method: 将生产级磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC移植到OpenMP，在NVIDIA A100（Leonardo Booster）和AMD MI250X（LUMI-G）设备上进行性能对比分析，进行内核级性能剖析以识别性能瓶颈。

Result: NVIDIA平台上OpenACC和OpenMP性能相当，共享编译器后端提供一致的基线。AMD MI250X上应用级性能下降约3倍，内核级下降最高达一个数量级（特定情况下47倍）。主要瓶颈是内存延迟受限而非带宽限制，低并行度内核中C++抽象层增加寄存器压力导致严重性能下降。

Conclusion: 跨GPU架构的可移植性能不仅需要应用层修改，还需要编译器后端的持续进步和架构感知的优化策略。当前编译器在处理跨步内存访问模式和C++抽象层优化方面存在局限性。

Abstract: The transition of scientific applications to GPU-accelerated exascale systems is constrained by trade-offs between performance, portability, and productivity. This work evaluates the performance portability of directive-based GPU programming by porting gPLUTO, a production-grade magnetohydrodynamics code for astrophysical simulations, from OpenACC to OpenMP, and analyzing its performance on NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) and AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) devices. On NVIDIA platforms, OpenACC and OpenMP achieve comparable performance due to a shared compiler backend, providing a consistent baseline for assessing algorithmic efficiency. In contrast, the same OpenMP implementation is approximately three times slower at the application level on AMD MI250X with respect to the NVIDIA A100 OpenACC baseline, with kernel-level slowdowns reaching up to an order of magnitude, driven by sensitivity to strided memory-access patterns and compiler limitations. Kernel-level profiling shows that the dominant contributors to run-time are memory-latency-bound rather than limited by peak band-width. In low-parallelism kernels, C++ abstraction layers increase register pressure and spilling, leading to extreme slowdowns of up to 47x in specific cases. These results indicate that portable performance across GPU architectures requires not only application-level changes but also continued advances in compiler backends and architecture-aware optimization strategies

</details>


### [145] [SemanticXR: Low Power and Real-time Queryable Semantic Mapping with an Object-Level Device-Cloud Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12849)
*Rahul Singh,Devdeep Ray,Connor Smith,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了SemanticXR，首个面向移动XR设备的实时开放词汇语义映射系统，通过对象级划分实现设备-云端协同，在满足功耗、带宽和内存约束下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义映射方法计算密集，需要服务器级资源。云卸载虽可行，但尚无系统能跨越设备-云边界划分语义映射或有效管理其通信、执行和内存占用。移动XR应用迫切需要开放词汇、实时且低功耗的语义映射能力。

Method: 核心创新是将语义可识别对象提升为通信、执行和内存的一等单元。服务器端采用对象级并行和几何下采样加速映射，对象级深度映射协同设计减少上行带宽；设备端采用对象级稀疏本地地图、增量更新和更新优先级机制，在有限内存和下行带宽下实现网络鲁棒的查询。系统提供可配置的资源-质量权衡以适应应用需求和运行条件。

Result: 与相同感知模型的设备-云基线相比，对象级组织使服务端映射延迟降低2.2倍（语义质量相同时）；深度映射协同设计保持上行带宽低于2.5 Mbps；设备端在10,000个对象规模下查询延迟低于100 ms（即使网络丢包），在500 MB内支持数万对象，下行带宽仅随地图变化而非场景总规模增长；正常操作仅增加2%设备功耗。

Conclusion: SemanticXR证明了对象级划分是实现资源受限移动XR设备实时语义映射的有效方法，在语义质量、延迟、带宽、内存和功耗之间取得了良好平衡，为XR应用提供了实用的语义映射解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic mapping is a core service that enables grounded interactions in emerging Extended Reality (XR) applications such as AI assistants and spatial object search. Deploying this capability on mobile XR devices requires a system that is open-vocabulary, real-time, and low-power. Existing approaches are compute-intensive and assume server-class resources. Cloud offloading offers a practical path, but no existing system splits semantic mapping across the device-cloud boundary or manages its communication, execution, and memory footprint.
  We present SemanticXR, the first device-cloud system for real-time, open-vocabulary semantic mapping and querying under XR power, bandwidth, and memory constraints. Our key insight is to elevate semantically identifiable objects to first-class units of communication, execution, and memory across the device and server. On the server, object-level parallelism and geometry downsampling improve mapping latency, while object-level depth-mapping co-design reduces upstream bandwidth. On the device, an object-level sparse local map with incremental updates and update prioritization enables network-robust querying with bounded memory and downstream bandwidth. Object-level configurable resource usage vs. quality trade-offs let applications and the system adapt mapping to application requirements and operating conditions, respectively.
  Against a device-cloud baseline with the same perception models, object-level organization improves server-side mapping latency by 2.2X at equal semantic quality. Depth-mapping co-design maintains upstream bandwidth under 2.5 Mbps. On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [146] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对ARM处理器的新型SME扩展，优化了谱元法波传播模拟软件SPECFEM3D，实现了4-6倍性能提升，并发现SME改变了性能最优的离散化参数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于谱元法(SEM)的波传播模拟是典型的高性能计算负载，但现有SEM实现未能有效适配具有可扩展矩阵扩展(SME)的新兴ARM多核CPU架构，存在性能优化空间。

Method: 提出三方面优化方案：(1)针对SME的批处理小矩阵内核优化SEM张量积算子；(2)面向有限HBM系统的内存感知混合MPI+OpenMP执行方案；(3)基于色散分析的(h,p)等精度权衡研究。在LX2处理器上进行实现和评估。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数下，优化实现相比原始代码获得4-6倍全应用性能提升，优于优化的非SME CPU基线。更重要的是，SME将性能最优工作点沿色散等精度边界推向更高阶多项式，进一步缩短求解时间并减少工作集大小。

Conclusion: SME不仅影响内核效率，还改变了现代ARM多核平台上SEM的实际离散化权衡，为高性能计算应用优化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [147] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Maestro，一个面向资源受限环境下大语言模型多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）的负载感知调度系统。通过利用智能体语义预测输出长度和内存使用，并采用分层调度策略，Maestro显著降低了KV预留的HBM使用（67.2%），并提升了高竞争场景下的SLO达成率（相比EDF提升23.6个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）已成为解决复杂任务的强大范式，但在资源受限的云环境中部署面临重大系统挑战：（1）每个用户查询会触发迭代的LLM调用流水线，资源消耗远超单轮查询；（2）解码阶段存在非确定性和输入依赖的成本；（3）多模型需求具有重尾特性，导致内存碎片化和过度配置；（4）跨集群调度存在权衡问题。

Method: Maestro采用负载感知的分层调度策略：（1）利用智能体语义和角色，预测每个阶段的输出长度和内存使用；（2）节点层面：通过分层权重缓存和弹性内存配置实现动态多模型共存；（3）集群层面：执行延迟感知路由，避免冷启动延迟和内存过载；（4）全局层面：实施工作流感知优先级调度，最小化交互式任务的队头阻塞。

Result: 通过原型实验和跟踪驱动仿真评估，Maestro相比基线方法取得了显著改进：（1）KV预留的HBM使用量减少67.2%；（2）在高竞争场景下，SLO达成率相比最早截止时间优先（EDF）调度提升23.6个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro成功解决了LLM-MAS在严格GPU预算下的服务部署难题。通过明确利用智能体语义进行预测驱动的分层调度，系统实现了高效的资源利用和服务质量保障，证明了语义感知调度在多智能体LLM系统中的重要价值。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks.
