Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [148] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Web3技术的联邦学习框架，通过分层架构和共识机制提高训练过程的透明度、可信度和可审计性，有效抵御对抗性和投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统主要部署在企业环境中，存在透明度有限和可审计性受限的问题。聚合过程不透明，难以判断哪些模型更新被接受或丢弃。当前的缓解策略通常依赖外部验证器，引入了额外的计算和通信开销，阻碍了联邦学习的广泛应用。

Method: 提出一个利用Web3技术的新型联邦学习框架，采用分层架构，由委托管理者在其各自的联盟内协调联邦学习训练过程。为缓解对抗性和投毒攻击，采用新颖性检测和共识机制的组合。模型更新被编码并广播给所有管理者，管理者独立评估其有效性，通过共识批准的模型更新被纳入全局模型。此外，采用基于声誉分数的备份机制确保模型生成。

Result: 在真实场景下进行的广泛实验证明了该框架的有效性和韧性，展示了其在传统企业环境之外实现透明联邦学习的潜力。框架成功抵御了对抗性和投毒攻击，通过共识机制和声誉系统确保了模型更新的可信度。

Conclusion: 该基于Web3的联邦学习框架能够有效提高训练过程的透明度、可信度和可审计性，为超越传统企业环境的透明联邦学习提供了可行的解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的MPI可塑性方法（DMR框架），可在生产HPC系统中动态调整资源，无需侵入式代码修改或调度器变更，在保持性能的同时显著降低节点小时消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学应用常表现出时变行为，但生产HPC集群仍依赖固定的资源分配方式，且大多数动态技术仅停留在实验室原型阶段，无法在实际生产环境中应用。

Method: 提出动态资源管理（DMR）框架，设计非侵入式MPI可塑性方法论，与现有HPC软件栈和运维实践集成，无需修改应用程序代码或资源调度器即可实现动态资源调整。

Result: 在三个TOP500超级计算机上部署两个大规模科学应用进行评估，结果表明该方法在受控环境中性能与静态基线相当，同时显著降低了相同工作负载的节点小时消耗。

Conclusion: 证明了使用标准资源管理器可以在生产系统上有效实现资源可塑性，降低了HPC中动态资源管理技术的采用门槛，为实际生产环境提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>


### [150] [Skiplists with Foresight: Skipping Cache Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13321)
*Tomer Cory,Niv Sulimany,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Foresight，一种针对跳表的缓存友好优化方法，可提升高达45%的吞吐量和15%的数据库端到端性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳表作为一种基础数据结构，在系统和应用中被广泛用于数据存储索引。然而，传统跳表设计在缓存利用率方面存在优化空间，缺乏针对缓存友好性的专门优化。

Method: 提出Foresight优化技术，这是一种针对跳表的缓存友好优化方法。该优化具有手术式精确性，易于集成到各种跳表设计中。针对并发环境扩展时引入的同步挑战进行了识别和解决。

Result: 在一个顺序跳表和三个并发跳表设计中应用Foresight，在微基准测试中观察到吞吐量提升高达45%。在DBx1000内存数据库中应用于基于跳表的索引时，获得了高达15%的端到端性能提升。

Conclusion: Foresight是一种有效的跳表缓存友好优化方法，易于集成且能带来显著的性能提升，适用于多种跳表设计和实际应用场景。

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [151] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了适用于稀疏网络的邻居窃取策略，限制工作窃取仅发生在直连邻居之间。实验表明其性能与全局窃取相当，且分析模型显示在星座规模增大时具有延迟优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步多任务（AMT）运行时假设全连接网络和低延迟，采用全局随机工作窃取策略。但空间边缘计算（SEC）中的低地球轨道卫星通过星间链路形成稀疏网格拓扑，远距离通信需要多跳，存在累积延迟问题。需要将AMT适配到SEC的稀疏网络环境。

Method: 提出邻居专用工作窃取策略，限制工作节点仅从直接连接的邻居处窃取任务，避免多跳通信。建立分析模型评估策略性能，并在HPC集群上构建模拟网格拓扑进行实验验证，在均匀低延迟链路下隔离受害者选择策略的影响。

Result: 分析模型表明限制窃取范围可获得单次尝试延迟优势，且优势随星座规模增大而增长。实验结果显示：在平衡和不规则工作负载下，邻居专用策略的性能与全局窃取策略相差约2.2%，表明限制受害者集合在此设置下不会损害负载平衡。

Conclusion: 邻居窃取策略可以达到与全局窃取策略相当的性能，且根据模型预测，在大规模系统上邻居窃取策略将更具优势，为AMT在空间边缘计算中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [152] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文引入了时序传导性Φ作为动态网络的连通性度量，将静态网络中投票模型的一致性时间上界推广到动态网络，并证明了该上界的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态传导性指标无法有效刻画动态网络的连通性（当某个时刻网络不连通时静态传导性会坍缩为0），需要一种能够捕获不同时刻出现的边所形成的连通性的新度量方法。

Method: 提出时序传导性Φ的概念来刻画动态网络的连通性，将Berenbrink等人基于静态传导性的投票模型一致性时间上界推广到时序传导性，并证明其紧性。

Result: 证明了标准投票模型在动态网络上的期望一致性时间上界为O(m/(d_min Φ))，其中m为边数，d_min为最小度，Φ为时序传导性；同时证明该上界在常数因子意义下是紧的。

Conclusion: 时序传导性是一个适用于动态网络分析的通用原语，可用于分析时序网络上的其他动力学过程，并在更广泛的时序非齐次马尔可夫链分析中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [153] [GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13501)
*Xinwei Qiang,Yifan Hu,Shixuan Sun,Jing Yang,Han Zhao,Chen Chen,Yu Feng,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了GF-DiT，一个面向Diffusion Transformer服务的弹性运行时系统，通过动态调整GPU并行度配置，相比静态并行方案实现了最高6.01倍的吞吐量提升和95%的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DiT服务系统为每个请求分配固定的并行配置，但DiT工作负载在请求、执行阶段和系统条件方面存在显著异构性，导致静态并行策略效率低下、GPU利用率差且服务质量下降。

Method: 提出GF-DiT策略可编程运行时系统：(1)引入异步执行抽象，将请求分解为独立可调度的轨迹任务，支持在线GPU重分配；(2)提出无集合体通信抽象(group-free collectives)，支持低开销的任意执行组在线形成与重配置。

Result: 在vLLM-Omni上实现并评估，相比静态并行方案：吞吐量提升最高6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高95%，SLO违规率降低最高90%，通信组建立开销从778ms降至约60μs。

Conclusion: 将GPU并行度作为一等可调度资源，通过弹性并行机制动态适应工作负载需求和服务目标，能够显著提升DiT服务的效率和服务质量。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality.
  This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups.
  We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.

</details>


### [154] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了await_delete构造扩展future语义以支持安全值重用，并基于此实现了对称矩阵分块求逆的future算法，在大规模问题上实现了近线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着并行工作负载复杂度增长，细粒度数据依赖管理成为关键挑战。Future模型虽然能处理不规则算法中的依赖关系，但其值不可变性限制了原地内存更新能力，而这正是高性能线性代数中内存复用的核心需求。

Method: 引入await_delete构造扩展传统future语义，允许消费者完成后安全地重用值；基于此扩展设计了密集对称矩阵分块求逆的新型future算法；在扩展版Taskflow中实现该方法。

Result: 通过强扩展性实验评估发现：future在小规模问题上产生显著开销，但在大规模矩阵上实现近线性扩展；分析了摊销阈值，证明future是大规模线性代数可行的高性能工具。

Conclusion: Future机制虽然在小规模问题上存在开销问题，但通过合理的摊销阈值分析，可以成为大规模线性代数计算的有效高性能工具，为细粒度依赖管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，揭示参数化工具检索模型存在知识-检索分离现象：在真实模糊查询下性能骤降50-64个百分点，且部分模型在事实探针测试中表现接近随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准测试使用冗长、完全指定的查询，且采用约束解码，无法揭示模型是否真正理解工具语义；需要更现实的评估方法来诊断参数化工具检索模型的真实能力。

Method: 提出ToolSense框架，输入工具目录后自动生成三类基准测试：包含三级模糊度的真实检索基准(RRB)、多选题探针基准和问答探针基准；在ToolBench数据集(约47k工具)上评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB查询下，多个配置相比完全指定的ToolBench基准性能下降50-64个百分点，甚至低于嵌入模型基线；尽管检索性能强劲，部分模型在事实探针上得分接近随机，揭示知识-检索分离现象。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索模型存在知识-检索分离问题，在真实模糊场景下性能严重退化；ToolSense框架开源，为工具检索模型提供更全面的诊断评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [156] [Reducing the Complexity of Deep Learning Models for EEG Analysis on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12742)
*Farough Shayeste Roodi,Parham Zilouchian Moghaddam,Mahdi Mohammadi-nasab,Mehdi Modarressi,Mostafa Ersali Salehi Nasab,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署最先进深度神经网络(DNN)模型的可行性，通过参数量化和电极减少方法，在保持较高准确率的同时显著降低了模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴医疗设备是物联网领域增长最快的领域，许多自动化医疗依赖ECG和EEG信号。然而深度神经网络的高计算、能耗和内存带宽需求与可穿戴设备严格的资源和能耗约束之间存在矛盾，阻碍了深度学习在实际可穿戴服务中的部署。

Method: 针对脑电信号分析(特别是癫痫检测)的多个最先进DNN模型，研究参数量化和电极减少方法对模型准确性和计算复杂度之间权衡关系的影响。

Result: 合理应用量化和电极减少技术可以在对准确率影响最小的情况下显著降低DNN模型的复杂度，揭示了在可穿戴设备上部署基于DNN的在线EEG分析时准确性与复杂度降低之间的明确权衡关系。

Conclusion: 证明了在可穿戴设备上部署最先进DNN模型的可行性，为资源受限环境下的深度学习模型优化提供了实用指导，填补了理论模型与实际部署之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Wearable healthcare devices are the fastest-growing Internet of Things (IoT) sector. Many automated healthcare services rely on two crucial biological signals, namely ECG and EEG, which reflect the activity of the heart and brain, respectively. Although deep neural networks are considered the primary way to process and analyze these signals, the very tight energy and computational power constraints in wearable devices are far below the computational, energy, and memory bandwidth demands of DNN models, thereby impeding the deployment of deep learning in many practical wearable services. This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying state-of-the-art DNN models in resource-constrained wearable devices. Notably, we explore the trade-off between accuracy and computational complexity of DNNs when parameter quantization and electrode reduction methods are used. Our investigation centers on several state-of-the-art DNN models designed for EEG signal analysis, specifically for detecting epileptic seizures. Our findings demonstrate that, when applied judiciously, these techniques can significantly reduce the complexity of the DNNs under consideration with minimal adverse effects on accuracy. These results reveal the explicit trade-offs between accuracy and complexity reduction encountered when adapting DNN-based online EEG analysis for wearable devices.

</details>


### [157] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，在LLM推理优化任务上实现了高达193%的性能提升，显著优于单智能体方案（仅33%且易崩溃）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主优化系统在隔离目标上运行，采用无状态评估，无法有效处理大规模、有状态动作空间中的复杂优化问题，特别是在需要跨应用、框架、编译器、内核和硬件栈协调优化的全栈LLM推理场景中。

Method: 提出了Arbor多智能体框架，核心创新包括：(1) 维护显式的评分假设搜索树作为共享工作记忆；(2) 采用制衡架构，包含负责优化协调的Orchestrator智能体和负责稳定性保障的Critic智能体；(3) 将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专业知识）和软技能（协调协议）；(4) 将失败视为诊断信号以重塑后续探索。

Result: 在LLM推理优化任务上，相比供应商优化基线实现了高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进；单智能体方案仅能实现33%提升且在数小时内不可恢复地崩溃；方法可推广至多代硬件平台，运行间方差在2个百分点以内，证明了硬件无关性和可复现性。

Conclusion: Arbor成功验证了结构化树搜索认知层在自主优化系统中的有效性，证明了多智能体制衡架构相比单智能体方案在复杂优化任务上的显著优势，为实现完全自主的多日优化活动提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [158] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AI智能体的战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用，同时控制遗漏支持错误，实现了在保证可靠性的前提下显著降低支持调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持研究关注人类如何使用机器学习模型做出更好决策，但在现代智能体系统中，角色发生逆转：AI智能体代表用户行动，而人类和工具成为支持机制。这种角色反转带来了可靠性问题，因为智能体错误可能产生严重后果，且智能体行为必须与人类目标和约束保持一致。

Method: 作者提出一个战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用，同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误（即智能体单独行动但支持本可以实质性改善其输出的概率）。证明了最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则。基于此结构，开发了在线算法来自适应设置阈值，并使用随机探索来控制遗漏支持错误，无需分布假设。还引入了即时校准方法来在线减少不必要的支持调用。

Result: 该框架在多种场景下进行了实例化，包括信息收集、人机协作和工具使用。实验结果表明，该方法能够可靠地控制目标错误，同时在实践中显著减少支持使用。证明了最优策略是支持价值上的阈值规则，并且在线算法能够在无分布假设的情况下有效运行。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将传统决策支持的两个基本原则（寻求支持的成本-价值权衡和不确定性量化的作用）重新应用到AI智能体作为核心行动者的场景中，为AI智能体的战略决策支持提供了理论框架和实用算法，有效平衡了可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [159] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pythagoras-Prover是一个高效的Lean定理证明器系列，通过课程学习、扩散模型和ALF数据增强，在显著减少参数量（4B模型比671B模型参数少167倍）的情况下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lean定理证明器需要大量训练和推理计算资源，主要受限于稀缺的验证证明数据和形式化证明搜索的长推理链，导致监督微调(SFT)和采样成本高昂。

Method: 1) 构建课程式SFT的分层Lean验证语料库（简单/中等/困难）；2) 提出动态证明推理过滤机制，保持8k token上下文预算；3) 引入ALF(Augmented Lean Formalisation)通过自蒸馏扩展验证语料库；4) 发布两类模型：自回归模型(4B/32B参数)和扩散式证明器(4B)。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B在MiniF2F-Test上pass@32达86.1%，超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B（82.4%）；32B模型达到开源SOTA 93.0%，解决PutnamBench中93/672问题；在MiniF2F-ALF污染敏感基准上所有模型准确率下降，但本方法仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 证明了通过课程学习、动态过滤和数据增强，可以在大幅降低计算成本的情况下构建高性能Lean定理证明器，并发布了新的污染敏感基准MiniF2F-ALF。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [160] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive通过检索风格指令下的人类驾驶数据来条件化VLA模型，实现了无需重新训练即可切换驾驶风格，在Bench2Drive上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶仿真器中的非智能体行为单一，缺乏风格多样性。近期工作虽通过后验标签或LLM推断的奖励权重引入风格变化，但这些只是代理信号，而非人类在明确风格指令下的真实驾驶演示。

Method: 三阶段流程：(i) 使用图文相似度对风格指令下的人类驾驶数据进行离线三元组挖掘；(ii) 训练轻量级检索头，融合冻结的视觉特征与小型控制编码器；(iii) 微调VLA骨干网络，将检索到的上下文点作为上下文行为演示用于航点预测。推理时通过切换不同风格的数据库实现风格条件化，无需重新训练。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上，PersonaDrive（无风格）相比SimLingo提升4.6%，相比HiP-AD提升2.5%；风格条件化下，所有风格均达最高驾驶分数，最弱风格仍比最强基线DMW高5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度提升18%，加速度提升25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive成功利用人类风格指令下的真实驾驶演示，实现了风格多样且无需重新训练的非智能体生成，为闭环仿真提供了更真实的交通环境。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [161] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有谎言检测器在提示欺骗场景下表现良好并随模型能力提升，但在训练过的欺骗性模型上性能急剧下降，无法支持关于模型信念的高置信度声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对语言模型的鲁棒谎言检测器能够支持模型行为的审计、监控和事后调查，但评估这些检测器需要模型确实持有与其陈述相反信念的测试平台，而现有训练模型往往无法满足此要求，导致先前的检测结果难以解释。

Method: 构建13个推理模型生物（其隐藏信念通过思维链验证并泛化到留存任务）和覆盖广泛欺骗动机的提示欺骗测试平台；评估四种检测器：思维链评判器、对数概率分类器、以及两种激活探针（包括新提出的DYL方法），在31个开源模型（2B-1T参数）上进行测试。

Result: 在提示欺骗场景下，所有四种检测器均随模型能力正向提升；但在训练模型生物上，激活和对数概率检测器性能急剧下降（DYL保留最多信号），仅思维链评判器保持0.82平衡准确率（部分归因于验证过程偏向CoT可读信念）。

Conclusion: 当前谎言检测器无法支持关于模型信念的高置信度声明，研究提出了可能解决其现有局限性的研究方向，并公开了数据集、模型生物和训练好的检测器。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [162] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TrajGenAgent，一个无需模型微调的语义感知分层LLM框架，通过两阶段的编排器-工作器设计生成高质量合成轨迹，在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体行为真实性方面优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹数据收集成本高且受隐私限制，亟需生成逼真的合成轨迹。现有LLM生成器存在权衡：提示工程方法缺乏细粒度时空基础，轨迹级微调方法计算成本高且可能削弱推理能力。

Method: 提出两阶段编排器-工作器框架：第一阶段LLM通过上下文学习合成个体和星期条件的活动链；第二阶段确定性工作流将活动落地为完整访问，包含个性化POI检索、距离感知位置选择、运动感知旅行时间传播和LLM时长估计。引入基于异常检测的评估框架评估行为和语义合理性。

Result: 在基准和大规模仿真数据集上，TrajGenAgent在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体特定行为真实性方面均优于代表性神经和LLM基线，同时避免了参数更新。

Conclusion: TrajGenAgent成功实现了无需微调的高质量轨迹生成，为交通、城市规划和疫情控制等领域提供了有效的数据增强解决方案，证明了分层LLM代理框架在时空数据生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [163] [The Illusion of Multi-Agent Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13003)
*Prathyusha Jwalapuram,Hehai Lin,Chuyuan Li,Fangkai Jiao,Sudong Wang,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Chengwei Qin,Giuseppe Carenini,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了多智能体系统（MAS）优于单智能体系统（SAS）的主流观点，发现自动生成的MAS在推理任务和交互式工作流中表现不如CoT-SC单智能体基线，且成本高出10倍；诊断实验揭示现有自动化设计范式产生架构臃肿，追求表面复杂度而非功能效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为MAS在上下文保护、并行处理和分布式决策方面优于SAS，但该结论基于优先考虑孤立推理任务的基准测试，未能充分评估MAS的优势。研究旨在通过严格系统评估验证自动生成的MAS是否真正优于单智能体基线。

Method: 1) 在传统推理数据集和交互式多步工作流任务（如BrowseComp-Plus）上系统评估自动生成的MAS与CoT-SC单智能体基线的性能；2) 构建诊断性合成数据集，具有显式任务分解、上下文分离和并行化潜力特征；3) 对比专家架构MAS与自动生成架构的性能与成本效率；4) 系统解构生成的MAS架构以识别设计缺陷。

Result: 自动生成的MAS在传统推理和交互式工作流任务中持续逊于CoT-SC，且成本高达10倍；专家架构的MAS在诊断数据集上性能和成本效率均优于自动生成架构；现有评估框架掩盖了复杂MAS的架构缺陷和低效性；当前自动化设计范式产生架构臃肿，追求表面复杂度而非功能效用，与多智能体原则存在根本性错位。

Conclusion: 主流观点对MAS优越性的支持缺乏充分实证基础，现有评估框架存在关键缺陷；当前自动MAS设计范式产生不必要的架构复杂性，需要重新审视MAS设计原则与评估方法，关注计算成本边际效用与功能效用的对齐。

Abstract: Prevailing wisdom posits that Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are superior to Single-Agent Systems (SAS), citing advantages like context protection, parallel processing and distributed decision-making. However, empirical support for this claim relies primarily on comparisons with SAS baselines using benchmarks that prioritize isolated reasoning tasks, which do not adequately assess these advantages. Focusing on automatically generated MAS that are designed for enhanced generalizability over manually-designed counterparts, we perform a rigorous, systematic evaluation against SAS, specifically Chain-of-Thought with Self-Consistency (CoT-SC). Across traditional reasoning datasets and tasks with interactive multi-step workflows (e.g., BrowseComp-Plus), we demonstrate that automatic MAS consistently underperform CoT-SC despite being up to 10x more expensive. To isolate these failures from limitations inherent to task structure, we introduce a diagnostic synthetic dataset tailored for MAS featuring explicit task decomposition, context separation and parallelization potential. We show that expert-architected MAS consistently outperforms automatically generated architectures in both raw performance and cost-efficiency on this dataset, demonstrating that existing evaluation frameworks mask critical architectural gaps and inefficiencies of complex MAS by failing to account for the marginal utility of increased computational cost. Critically, systematic deconstruction of the generated MAS architectures reveals that current automated design paradigms produce architectural bloat that prioritizes superficial complexity which does not translate into functional utility, exposing a fundamental misalignment with multi-agent principles.

</details>


### [164] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对小型语言模型在MCP工具调用场景中成功率极低的问题，本文提出推理时进化搜索方法Evoflux，通过结构化编辑和执行反馈修复工作流，将执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%，证明了执行导向的搜索比传统微调方法在稀缺数据条件下更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型语言模型虽然降低了成本和部署风险，但在MCP风格工具使用中面临严峻挑战：需要从动态目录发现工具、满足模式约束、维护中间输出依赖关系并基于执行证据生成响应。小型规划器常生成看似合理但执行失败的工作流，而少量轨迹蒸馏无法覆盖对变化工具目录进行失败恢复的行为需求。

Method: 提出Evoflux推理时进化搜索方法，将紧凑工具使用视为可执行工具工作流的修复过程。该方法通过结构化编辑、执行反馈、自适应强度、元引导重设计和多样性剪枝来演化类型化工作流图，在推理时动态调整和优化工作流。

Result: 在涵盖实时MCP服务器和250个工具的MCP-Bench保留任务上，Evoflux将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%。相比之下，基于相同搜索挖掘数据的SFT和SFT+DPO方法性能匹配、低于零样本表现甚至崩溃；ReAct虽能达到更高峰值，但方差更大且token成本更高。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在教师轨迹预算稀缺的情况下，执行导向的搜索方法比监督微调和强化学习微调更可靠，为紧凑型语言模型的工具使用能力提升提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [165] [LLM-as-an-Investigator: Evidence-First Reasoning for Robust Interactive Problem Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13220)
*Fabrizio Marozzo,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在技术问题诊断中过早迎合用户假设的问题，提出了LLM-as-an-Investigator方法。该方法通过证据优先的代理AI流程，先生成候选假设、提出针对性澄清问题并更新假设概率，直至证据充分才给出诊断，在机械、电气和液压领域的技术论坛基准测试中表现优于标准助手和推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在作为技术问题解决的交互式助手时，当用户提供不完整描述或未经证实的解释时，模型往往会过早与这些假设对齐，在没有收集充分证据的情况下就提出解决方案。这种行为被称为用户驱动的谄媚性（user-driven sycophancy）：LLM倾向于强化用户提供的假设，而不是测试替代解释。这种倾向会导致诊断不准确，因此需要一种能够系统性地收集证据、测试多种假设的方法。

Method: 提出LLM-as-an-Investigator方法，这是一种证据优先的代理AI方法论。核心是Solution Investigator Agent，其工作流程包括：（1）评估初始问题描述的模糊性；（2）生成候选假设；（3）提出针对性澄清问题；（4）根据每个答案更新假设概率。代理不会立即产生响应，而是继续调查直到证据使某一候选解释明显强于替代方案。评估方面，构建了来自机械、电气和液压领域已解决技术论坛帖子的基准，采用三代理评估流程：Problem-Solution Extractor Agent将已解决帖子转换为结构化案例，Ground-Truth Evaluator Agent模拟用户并隐藏已知解决方案，被测助手通过对话尝试恢复解决方案。

Result: 实验比较了标准助手、面向推理的LLM和所提出的基于调查员的模型。结果表明：（1）所提出的方法在诊断准确性上优于直接提示和仅推理基线；（2）证据优先协议有助于减少用户诱导的对话偏差；（3）分析了标准助手在诊断案例中如何跟随误导性用户假设。该方法在识别问题方面更加准确，同时减少了对话中的偏见。

Conclusion: LLM-as-an-Investigator方法通过证据优先的调查协议，有效缓解了用户驱动的谄媚性问题，提升了技术诊断的准确性和可靠性。这为构建更鲁棒的问题诊断AI助手提供了有效的方法论框架，证明了在技术领域应用中采用系统性证据收集和假设测试的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive assistants for technical problem solving. However, when users provide incomplete descriptions or plausible but unverified explanations, LLMs may prematurely align with these assumptions and propose solutions before collecting sufficient evidence. We refer to this behavior as user-driven sycophancy: the tendency of an LLM to reinforce a user-provided hypothesis instead of testing alternative explanations. This paper introduces LLM-as-an-Investigator, an evidence-first agentic AI methodology for robust problem diagnosis. The approach is implemented through a Solution Investigator Agent, which estimates the ambiguity of an initial problem description, generates candidate hypotheses, asks targeted clarification questions, and updates hypothesis probabilities after each answer. Rather than producing an immediate response, the agent continues the investigation until the evidence makes one candidate explanation stronger than the alternatives. To evaluate the approach, we build a benchmark from solved technical forum threads in mechanical, electrical, and hydraulic domains. We use a three-agent evaluation pipeline in which a Problem-Solution Extractor Agent converts solved threads into structured cases, a Ground-Truth Evaluator Agent simulates the user while hiding the known solution, and the tested assistant attempts to recover the solution through dialogue. The experiments compare standard assistants, reasoning-oriented LLMs, and the proposed investigator-based model across LLM backbones. In addition to diagnostic accuracy, we analyze how standard assistants follow misleading user hypotheses in diagnostic cases. The results show that the proposed approach identifies the problem more accurately than direct prompting and reasoning-only baselines, while its evidence-first protocol helps reduce user-induced conversational bias.

</details>


### [166] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本报告探讨了从通用人工智能(AGI)向人工通用超级智能(ASI)过渡的路径、潜在瓶颈及研究问题，指出AI进步可能持续加速，带来一系列社会变革而非单一剧变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AGI从科幻推测变为许多大型AI组织的具体目标，其对人类社会的深远影响引发诸多复杂问题。本研究旨在探讨AGI之后AI如何在机器智能连续谱上继续发展，特别关注从人类水平AGI向超级智能(ASI)的过渡。

Method: 首先描述ASI特征（比大型人类组织更智能的认知系统），然后分析从AGI到ASI的四种潜在路径：(1)扩大AGI规模，(2)AI范式转变，(3)递归改进，(4)大规模多智能体集体涌现ASI。随后讨论这些路径中可能的摩擦与瓶颈，并提出具体的开放研究问题。

Result: 由于预测ASI进展存在巨大不确定性，无法排除AI进步在未来几年持续加速的可能性。这意味着将AGI引入社会引发的单一变革性阶跃可能不准确，更可能是AI驱动的科学和技术突破引发一系列社会变革。

Conclusion: 应对这一前景需要全球范围内大规模的跨学科协作努力，这为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead. This report investigates how AI itself might continue to develop in a post-AGI world along the continuum of machine intelligence. The endpoint of this continuum, Universal AI, is theoretically well understood, which provides some formal grounding for the main focus of this report: the transition from human-level AGI to artificial general superintelligence, which, intuitively, can be understood as a system that is more intelligent and cognitively capable than large organisations of humans. After characterizing ASI, the report discusses four potential pathways from AGI to ASI: scaling AGI, AI paradigm shifts, recursive improvement, and ASI emerging from large-scale multi-agent collectives. The report then discusses possible frictions and bottlenecks along these pathways. Determining whether the impact of these frictions will be negligible or substantial raises a number of concrete open research questions. Due to large uncertainties for predicting ASI progress, it cannot be ruled out that AI progress might continue to accelerate over the next years. This could imply that the image of a single transformative step change, caused by the introduction of human-level AGI into our society, could be inaccurate. More apt might be the prospect of a series of transformative societal changes caused by AI-enabled progress and breakthroughs across many areas of science and technology. Preparing for this prospect requires a massively interdisciplinary endeavour of global scope and interest.

</details>


### [167] [Can I Buy Your KV Cache?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13361)
*Luoyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出预计算KV缓存复用机制，实现24/24 token精确匹配，计算成本降低9-50倍，为流行文档节省数百万美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理重复执行预填充计算相同文档，造成大量计算资源浪费。每个代理都独立计算并重建相同的KV缓存，效率极低。

Method: 发布者预计算文档的KV缓存，其他代理付费加载并跳过预填充。采用提供商端托管方式避免网络传输成本，消除KV缓存不可压缩带来的数据传输开销。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上验证了token级别精确匹配（24/24贪婪token），计算复用成本比预填充低9-50倍。向80M代理提供3774词文档：预填充成本约150万美元，复用成本仅约3万美元（节省49.7倍）。

Conclusion: 构建了代理原生预填充CDN框架，实现了显著的成本节约。KV缓存无损压缩和跨方支付层仍为开放问题，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Right now, across the world, AI agents are repeating the same absurd act: to read one document, they each recompute it from scratch. Every agent re-runs prefill, the most compute-intensive step a large model takes, over identical text, only to rebuild a key-value (KV) cache identical to the one the agent before it just built. The same answer, computed a million times. We make a proposal that is almost offensively simple: compute it once. Let a publisher precompute a document's KV cache, and let every other agent buy the right to load it and skip prefill. It works, and it is token-exact: loading a precomputed KV and continuing matches prefilling from scratch (24/24 greedy tokens, and at the logits level), with no accuracy cost. On Qwen3-4B, reuse is 9-50x cheaper in compute than prefill, and the gap widens with length (prefill's attention scales with L^2), so a single reuse already pays it back. Then the part that matters: where the KV lives. Shipping it fails, because KV is nearly incompressible, so per-load egress costs more than the prefill it saves. Hosting it provider-side, exactly as production prompt-caching works, removes egress entirely. The size of the prize is set by our measured compute saving: serving one hot 3774-token document to 80M agents costs ~$1.5M to re-prefill but only ~$0.03M of reuse compute (49.7x less). The 0.1x cache-read tariff APIs charge passes a 10x discount to users while sitting inside this measured envelope, so the 10x is a floor that the measured ~50x compute saving clears, and the gap to the physical ~50x is provider margin: millions of dollars per popular document. We frame the resulting agent-native prefill CDN and leave lossless KV compression and a cross-party payment layer as the open problems.

</details>


### [168] [Deployment-Centered Evaluation: Predicting Query-Level Rejection Risk in a Clinical LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12702)
*Alyssa Unell,Miguel Fuentes,Brenna Li,Bridget Lin,Meena Jagadeesan,Sanmi Koyejo,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究在学术医疗中心的电子健康记录系统中部署大语言模型，通过训练预响应分类器预测用户是否会拒绝LLM响应。利用部署特定上下文（如提供者类型、部门名称、所用语言模型）而非仅依赖查询内容，模型在4.5个月的用户反馈前瞻性分析中达到0.719的AUROC，证明了预测用户拒绝的可行性，为针对性护栏机制奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态基准测试主要衡量正确性而非用户接受度，聚合查询性能，且需要密集标注数据集，导致临床系统评估存在重大盲点。由于LLM越来越多地集成到临床系统中，评估这些系统的实际效用至关重要。传统评估方法难以反映真实部署条件下的用户反馈，因此需要一种能够预测用户拒绝响应的方法来识别潜在问题。

Method: 在学术医疗中心的电子健康记录系统中嵌入LLM系统，训练预响应分类器，基于查询内容和部署特定上下文（提供者类型、部门名称、用于响应的语言模型）来估计未来交互导致用户拒绝LLM响应的风险。进行4.5个月的前瞻性分析，并评估预测在两个下游用例（护栏触发和弃权）中的效益。

Result: 预测模型在4.5个月的用户反馈前瞻性分析中达到AUROC为0.719。关键发现是利用部署特定上下文而非仅依赖查询内容，能够显著提高预测用户是否会拒绝系统输出的能力。研究估计了此类预测在护栏触发和弃权两个下游用例中的效益。

Conclusion: 实证案例研究表明，使用部署特定上下文预测用户拒绝是可行的。这一发现为针对性护栏机制打开了大门，可以提前识别潜在的用户拒绝风险并采取相应措施，从而提高临床LLM系统的实用性和用户接受度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical systems, making it essential to evaluate the real-world utility of these systems. However, static benchmarks tend to measure correctness rather than user acceptance, aggregate performance across queries, and require densely annotated datasets -- leading to major blind spots for evaluating clinical systems. In this work, we perform a deployment-centered evaluation of an LLM system embedded within electronic health records at an academic medical center, where user feedback is sparse but closely reflects the deployment conditions. Specifically, we train a pre-response classifier that estimates the risk that a future interaction will result in the user rejecting the LLM response, based on query content and deployment-specific context available before generation. We conduct a prospective analysis of our model over 4.5 months of user feedback, finding that our prediction model achieves an AUROC of 0.719. Further, we estimate the benefit of such predictions in two downstream use cases (guardrail triggering and abstention). Our key conceptual insight is that making use of deployment-specific context (i.e., the provider type, department name, language model used for response), as opposed to only query content, improves the ability to predict whether the user will reject the system output. Altogether, our empirical case study demonstrates the feasibility of predicting user rejection using deployment-specific context, opening the door to targeted guardrails.

</details>


### [169] [Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“合规性构造”范式，将领域内的符号结构作为LLM智能体的核心架构组件，以预防控制流违规，并与传统的基于护栏的监控互补，共同确保受监管行业流程自动化的合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体逐渐应用于受监管行业并自动化执行涉及判断的质量管理流程，现有的仅将符号结构（如法规、流程模型）视为外部监控机制的做法已不足够。为了在关键领域确保安全与合规，需要将这些符号结构提升为智能体决策与行为的核心架构组件。

Method: 提出了一种名为“合规性构造”的互补范式。该范式将符号结构作为结构基础以预防控制流违规，同时保留基于护栏的监控以捕捉语义错误。作者在基础与能力层面识别了一组神经符号研究挑战，并指出联合解决这些挑战可实现合规性构造。

Result: 成功构建了合规性构造的理论框架，并识别出实现该框架所需解决的关键神经符号研究挑战。结果表明，通过将合规性构造与护栏监控相结合，可以为受监管流程自动化提供更可靠的合规保障。

Conclusion: 合规性构造是神经符号智能体在受监管流程自动化领域的重要新范式，它将合规性内建于系统架构而非仅依赖外部监控。作者呼吁神经符号社区将该领域作为具有高影响力的研究方向进行深入探索。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering regulated industries where they automate judgment intensive quality management processes. We argue that symbolic structures already embedded in these domains, including regulations, typed process models, and compliance constraints, should be treated not merely as external monitoring mechanisms but as core architectural components that shape the agent's decision-making and behavior. We propose compliance-by-construction as a complementary paradigm to guardrail-based monitoring: a structural foundation that prevents control-flow violations, while guardrails remain essential for catching semantic errors. We identify a structured set of neuro-symbolic research challenges on foundational and capability level and show that addressing them jointly enables compliance-by-construction. We call on the neuro-symbolic community to engage with regulated process automation as a high impact research domain.

</details>


### [170] [Definitional alignment before capability alignment: a Design-Science framework for adjudicating claims about AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12713)
*J. E. Aguilera Briones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AGI概念缺乏统一定义的问题，开发了DAF-AGI概念框架，包含五项评判定义适用性的标准和治理审计机制，并用于测试当前生成式系统是否构成AGI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于AGI是否已经实现的争论往往基于重叠证据，但"AGI"缺乏单一、稳定、共享的定义指称，不同操作化方式可能对同一系统给出不同判断。这种定义的不明确性被视为设计和治理问题。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法论，开发了DAF-AGI二阶概念框架，包含两个耦合组件：(1)五项序数标准用于评估候选定义的裁决适用性；(2)针对作者身份、利益、认证、外部验证和修订权限的结构化治理审计。将此框架应用于五大测量家族和一个紧缩边界立场进行演示和压力测试。

Result: 在2024-2025年来源证据下，当前生成系统构成AGI的主张仅在基于性能的操作化下可被认证；能力本体论、心理测量和技能获取方法均未认证该主张，经济家族仍为不确定状态，紧缩立场拒绝二元裁决。

Conclusion: 提出了定义主权作为算法主权的组成部分，即制度性能力在公共问责下质疑、认证和修订进口技术类别。贡献在于新颖的整合与操作化，仍需独立应用、评估者间测试和作者外部案例进行验证。

Abstract: Claims that artificial general intelligence has already arrived and claims that it remains decades away are often defended from overlapping evidence. "AGI" lacks a single shared and stable referent and competing operationalizations can return different verdicts on the same system. This article treats that under-specification as a design and governance problem. Following Design Science Research Methodology, it develops DAF-AGI, a second-order conceptual artifact with two coupled components: five ordinal criteria for assessing the adjudicative fitness of candidate definitions and a structured governance audit of authorship, interest, certification, external verification and revision authority. The artifact is demonstrated on five prominent measurement families and one deflationary boundary position in a documented corpus and then stress-tested against a stylized strong arrival claim: that current generative systems constitute AGI because they outperform a well-educated adult on many cognitive tasks. On evidence from the cited 2024-2025 sources, the claim was certifiable only under a performance-based operationalization; capability-ontology, psychometric and skill-acquisition approaches did not certify it, the economic family remains indeterminate and the deflationary position refuses binary adjudication. The contribution is a novel integration and operationalization, not an empirical validation: independent application, inter-rater testing and author-external cases remain necessary. The paper further proposes definitional sovereignty as an enabling component of algorithmic sovereignty: the institutional capacity to contest, certify and revise imported technological categories under public accountability.

</details>


### [171] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对多智能体辩论系统的置信度聚合方法，通过信号转换与软投票/贝叶斯融合两种协议，在保持正确率的同时显著提升了置信度的判别能力（AUARC），并恢复了MAD在歧义任务上的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然语言处理系统中，置信度被广泛用于可靠性评估、监督及下游决策任务。然而，目前没有任何方法能够为多智能体系统输出一个聚合的系统级置信度。虽然已有研究在多智能体辩论中使用置信度进行消息加权、触发辩论或校准个体智能体，但都未将这些信号整合为系统整体的单一致信度，这一空白限制了多智能体系统在关键决策场景中的应用。

Method: 提出了三种协议来生成最终答案及其聚合置信度：1) 将原始置信度信号进行转换，使其在不同模型间具有可比性；2) 采用软投票或一种称为“贝叶斯融合”的概率融合方法来组合这些信号；3) 比较分析了两种估计器（序列概率和自报告）与参数化及非参数化校准器的效果。实验涵盖了五个基准、四种任务类型、六组同质和异质辩论对，跨越不同模型能力和规模。

Result: 聚合置信度在判别性（AUARC指标）上显著优于最佳单一智能体和标准辩论基线；正确性（F1分数）保持稳定，并恢复了MAD在更模糊任务上造成的性能损失；校准提升了两种估计器的F1分数，而AUARC对校准的依赖较少。该方法成功生成了多智能体系统的系统级置信度，填补了该领域的技术空白。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现了多智能体辩论系统的置信度聚合，证明了通过适当的信号转换和融合方法可以获得比单一智能体更具判别力的系统级置信度，同时保持甚至改善任务性能。这一方法为多智能体系统在需要可靠置信度评估的关键应用场景中铺平了道路。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [172] [The Theory of Mind Utility: Formal Specification of a Mentalizing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12721)
*Nikolos Gurney,Stacy Marsella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了心智理论效用(ToM-U)框架，通过构建局部认知世界模型(LEWMs)来解决推断他人信念的复杂问题，能够追踪信息传递的来源、顺序和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推断他人信念需要追踪谁告诉了他们什么、按什么顺序以及可信度如何，这一认识状态推断问题缺乏计算层面的正式理论框架。现有方法要么预设而非推导信念状态（如贝叶斯心智理论），要么缺乏认识状态推断的形式化机制（如模拟理论和理论-理论）。

Method: 构建局部认知世界模型(LEWMs)——有向类型化图结构，表示智能体、状态节点及其间的认识关系。提出五个形式化定义：LEWM结构、智能体节点属性（包括有序信息访问历史）、有界增殖机制（用于递归心智化）、三种推断程序，以及捕获失败心智化尝试的结构化痕迹的残差函数。通过评估离散候选LEWMs与观察行为的匹配度直到获得足够置信度。

Result: ToM-U框架能够生成关于心智化失败的定向、可证伪预测，这些预测源自模型的结构特性而非辅助假设。该架构将ToM-U定位为目标推断和其他下游社会认知过程上游的领域无关机制。

Conclusion: ToM-U在计算分析层面形式化了认识状态推断问题，明确心智化计算什么以及为什么，而不涉及算法或神经实现的承诺，为心智理论研究提供了新的理论框架和可检验预测。

Abstract: Inferring others' beliefs requires more than reading surface signals; it requires tracking who told them what, in what order, and how credibly. The Theory of Mind Utility (ToM-U) formalizes this epistemic state inference problem at the computational level of analysis, specifying what mentalizing computes and why without commitment to algorithmic or neural implementation. ToM-U achieves this by constructing Local Epistemic World Models (LEWMs) -- directed typed graphs that represent agents, state nodes, and the epistemic relationships among them -- and evaluating discrete candidate LEWMs against observed behavior until one achieves sufficient confidence. Five formal definitions specify the LEWM structure, agent node properties including ordered information access history, a bounded proliferation mechanism for recursive mentalizing, three inference procedures, and a residue function that captures the structured trace left by failed mentalizing attempts. ToM-U differs from Bayesian Theory of Mind and adjacent formal accounts, which presuppose rather than derive belief states, and from simulation theory and theory-theory, which lack a formal apparatus for epistemic state inference. The architecture generates directional, falsifiable predictions about mentalizing failure that follow from structural properties of the model rather than auxiliary assumptions, and positions ToM-U as a domain-agnostic mechanism upstream of goal inference and other downstream social cognitive processes.

</details>


### [173] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OrchRM，一种自监督的编排奖励建模框架，无需人工标注即可评估多智能体系统的编排质量，在训练效率上提升10倍，测试时扩展准确率提升8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）需要有效的编排来协调专业化智能体，但编排器的训练受限于监督信号稀缺和高昂的计算成本。现有的MAS测试时扩展和编排器训练框架依赖昂贵的子智能体rollout，效率低下。

Method: 提出编排奖励建模（OrchRM）自监督框架：利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构建胜负对（win-lose pairs），用于Bradley-Terry奖励模型训练。与现有方法不同，OrchRM直接在编排层面操作，无需依赖代价高昂的子智能体rollout，实现了高效的奖励引导编排器训练。

Result: OrchRM在令牌使用上提升训练效率高达10倍，同时将MAS测试时扩展的准确率提升高达8%。这些性能增益在数学推理、基于网页的问答和多跳推理等多个领域均表现出一致的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 编排层面的奖励建模是构建鲁棒多智能体编排的可扩展方向，OrchRM证明了无需人工标注即可实现高效、高性能的多智能体系统编排器训练和测试时扩展。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [174] [Rethinking Psychometric Evaluation of LLMs: When and Why Self-Reports Predict Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12730)
*Rafal Kocielnik,Pengrui Han,Peiyang Song,Myrl G. Marmarelis,Ramit Debnath,Dean Mobbs,Anima Anandkumar,R. Michael Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM的自我报告与行为之间存在选择性一致性：在共享对话中，特定行为意图测量(TPB)可达到人类水平的一致性，而宽泛的人格特质(Big 5)则不能；跨会话时一致性仅存在于训练形成的隐式偏见等行为上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现LLM存在显著的自我报告与行为分离现象，但该研究依赖于宽泛的大五人格特质(即使对人类行为预测力也较弱)，且会话隔离和弱上下文匹配使得无法确定LLM是否真正缺乏一致性。作者旨在探索更精细的理论框架能否更好预测LLM行为。

Method: 对比大五人格(Big 5)与计划行为理论(TPB)两种测量框架，后者针对特定行为测量意图。在4个行为任务和11个前沿LLM上进行实验，同时变化会话上下文和身份诱导条件。

Result: 发现自我报告-行为一致性存在但具有选择性：(1)共享对话内，TPB达到人类水平一致性，Big 5未达到；(2)跨独立对话时，一致性仅存于训练塑造的隐式偏见等行为，在强烈情境引导(如谄媚行为)时崩溃；(3)人格提示使跨会话自我报告更一致，但未使行为对齐。

Conclusion: 粗粒度人格框架(如Big 5)可能不是测试部署行为的最佳工具，需要更任务特异和行为特异的测量工具，且需跨任务和情境评估。

Abstract: Anticipating LLM behavioral tendencies from low-cost psychometric probes is critical for safe deployment, but only if self-reports (SR) reliably predict behavior. Recent work documented substantial SR-behavior dissociation in LLMs, but relied on broad personality traits (Big 5) that predict specific behaviors weakly, even in humans. Furthermore, the isolation of conversational sessions combined with weak context matching left open whether LLMs truly lack coherence or whether the conditions needed to detect such coherence were not met. We contrast Big 5 with the Theory of Planned Behavior (TPB), which measures intention targeted to a specific behavior and predicts human behavior substantially better than broad traits. We run experiments across four behavioral tasks and 11 frontier LLMs, while also varying session context and identity induction. We find that SR-behavior coherence exists but is selective. 1) Within a shared conversation, the Theory of Planned Behavior reaches human-level coherence; Big 5 does not. 2) Across separate conversations, coherence survives only for behaviors anchored outside the immediate prompt, such as implicit bias shaped by training, and collapses when behavior is strongly primed by context, as with sycophancy. 3) Persona prompting makes self-reports more consistent across conversations, but does not bring behavior into alignment. These findings suggest that coarse personality frameworks, such as Big 5 may not be the best tools for testing deployment behavior. More task- and behavior-specific instruments are needed, and even these must be evaluated across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [175] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DoorDash部署的一种强化学习系统，通过学习自实际市场数据的离线策略来调整派单优化器的目标权重，在保持配送质量的同时提高了 batching 效率并降低了配送员时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三方市场中的派单决策面临延迟运营结果评估（如配送速度、配送员利用率、商家拥堵）的挑战，这为强化学习提供了自然的反馈机制。目标是利用这些来自真实经济物流系统的"世界反馈"来安全地在线适应决策策略。

Method: 采用集中式离线数据训练共享价值函数与分散式店铺级执行的架构。使用 Double Q-learning 目标和保守正则化器减少分布外价值高估。学习到的店铺级策略选择离散乘数，调整派单优化器在配送质量和 batching 效率之间的权衡，而非直接替代组合分配优化器。该方法保留了生产可行性约束和运营保障。

Result: 在生产环境的 switchback 实验中，离线训练的策略在面向客户的配送质量无下降的情况下，增加了 batching 并降低了配送员侧的时间成本。这证明了通过延迟信号适应大规模食品配送市场调度目标权重的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究展示了如何利用来自真实经济物流系统的世界反馈安全地在线适应决策策略，验证了在存在噪声、延迟和耦合反馈的复杂生产环境中应用离线强化学习的有效性。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [176] [Benchmarking AI Agents for Addressing Scientific Challenges Across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12736)
*Tianyu Liu,Allen Xin Wang,Antonia Panescu,Lisa Xinyi Chen,Wenxin Long,Xinyu Wei,Yueqian Jing,Ziyao Zeng,Jihang Chen,Sihan Jiang,Ziqing Wang,Siyi Gu,Siyu Chen,Xinyang Hu,Haoran Shao,Leqi Xu,Wangjie Zheng,Zhiyuan Cao,Ada Fang,Botao Yu,Kunyang Sun,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan,Qingyu Chen,Lingzhou Xue,Kaize Ding,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin,Zhuoran Yang,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了SciAgentArena，一个包含约200个任务的科学AI智能体评测基准。研究发现当前智能体在明确的数据分析工作流中表现良好，但在开放性研究问题中仍面临生成新见解、自主探索和构建稳健解决方案的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体评测基准无法捕捉科学研究的复杂性、异构性和长期推理需求，而科学任务评测基准又往往将研究简化为静态的、直接的问题，缺乏交互式评估支持，因此亟需一个能够真实反映科研场景的系统性评测框架。

Method: 提出SciAgentArena评测基准，包含约200个真实科研场景任务，提供逐步验证机制和交互式的、智能体无关的评估环境，支持对多样化AI智能体的系统性评估。

Result: 当前AI智能体在结构清晰、评估标准明确的数据分析工作流中能够有效贡献；但在不同科学语境下表现不均，难以生成真正的新见解、维持自主探索，且在处理开放性研究问题时难以构建稳健解决方案。研究还总结了常见失败模式并提出了改进方向。

Conclusion: SciAgentArena为科学AI智能体的进展评估提供了实用框架，能够指导未来智能体设计，使其更好地应对复杂科学挑战，提升智能体在科学研究中的可靠性、自主性和科学推理能力。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed to accelerate scientific discovery, yet their practical capabilities in real research settings remain poorly understood. Existing benchmarks for AI agents rarely capture the complexity, heterogeneity, and extended reasoning required by scientific work, whereas benchmarks for scientific tasks often reduce research to static, direct problems and provide limited support for interactive evaluation. Here, we introduce SciAgentArena, a systematic benchmark for evaluating AI agents in real-world scientific research scenarios drawn from emerging needs across multiple domains. SciAgentArena comprises approximately 200 tasks with stepwise verification and an interactive, agent-agnostic environment for assessing diverse AI agents. Using this benchmark, we find that current agents can contribute effectively to well-specified data-analysis workflows, particularly when the task structure and evaluation criteria are clear. However, their performance remains uneven across scientific contexts: agents struggle to generate genuinely novel insights, sustain self-directed exploration, and formulate robust solutions for open-ended research questions. We further characterize common failure modes across agents and identify opportunities for improving their reliability, autonomy, and scientific reasoning. Together, SciAgentArena provides a practical framework for measuring progress in AI agents for science and for guiding the design of future agents capable of addressing complex scientific challenges. Full codes, tasks, and datasets can be accessed via this link: https://sciagentarena.github.io/.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新定位了“屏蔽强化学习”：不应将其视为运行时安全机制，而应作为设计时的分析工具。通过约束双人安全博弈对网络防御进行形式化分析，输出可防御性裁决及相关指标，为架构设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍将屏蔽强化学习视为一种运行时安全机制，通过编译时序逻辑规范为自动机来约束智能体动作。作者认为这种视角错位，指出同一套自动机理论工具更适合作为设计时的分析手段，用于产生关于系统的结构性洞见，而非部署时的运行时约束。

Method: 构建一个约束双人安全博弈用于网络防御。两条规范的执行不对称：防御者规范定义博弈的不安全区域，攻击者规范在吸引子计算过程中约束对手的合法动作。通过求解博弈获得可防御性裁决、获胜区域与屏蔽策略。进一步从吸引子结构提取拓扑级指标，并与屏蔽约束下的对抗性多智能体强化学习的收敛后行为相结合，形成可防御性指纹。

Result: 方法输出包括：可防御性裁决（网络拓扑—规范对是否可防御的形式化证书）、获胜区域与屏蔽策略、基于吸引子结构的拓扑级指标、以及结合强化学习行为的可防御性指纹。反事实分析显示，形式可防御性与运营有效性捕捉了安全的不同维度：小规模架构变化可导致运营结果大幅变化而形式安全边界几乎不变。

Conclusion: 屏蔽合成的价值不在于作为安全智能体的部署机制，而在于作为回答系统是否可防御、在何处可防御以及如何可防御等架构问题的框架。可防御性裁决才是核心输出，而非安全策略本身。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [178] [Prefill Awareness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12747)
*Andy Wang,Parv Mahajan,David Demitri Africa,Alexandra Souly,Jordan Taylor,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 前沿语言模型具备显著的“预填充感知”能力，能识别助手侧上下文是否被篡改，这对依赖预填充的安全研究方法（如对齐和越狱评估）构成潜在干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的安全相关研究（包括对齐、越狱评估和AI控制协议）常依赖预填充模型输出。如果AI模型能识别并对其先前的助手消息被插入或编辑这一事实做出反应，这些方法的有效性和有效性可能会受到损害。因此需要调查前沿语言模型是否具备区分篡改和未篡改助手侧上下文的能力。

Method: 构建了一个二进制偏好基准测试，涵盖三种预填充机制，筛选出模型表现出一致立场的案例。通过受控消融实验分离不同线索的影响，并在更现实的代理设置（如错位延续评估和SWE-bench轨迹）中进行验证。

Result: 前沿模型表现出显著的预填充感知能力：Claude Opus 4.5在提示下能以0%的假阳性率检测出9-35%的反对其偏好的预填充；模型常在不明确报告预填充为外来的情况下恢复到基线行为。风格不匹配主要影响模型是否将预填充标记为外来，而偏好不匹配主要影响它们是否恢复到基线答案。

Conclusion: 预填充感知已成为某些基于预填充方法的实质性混杂因素。建议模型开发者在前沿系统中跟踪这一能力，以确保相关安全评估方法的有效性。

Abstract: Safety-relevant studies of language models, including alignment and jailbreaking evaluations and AI control protocols, often rely on prefilling model outputs. If AI models can recognize and act on the fact their prior assistant messages have been inserted or edited, the effectiveness and validity of these methods could be compromised. We investigate whether frontier language models can distinguish between tampered and untampered assistant-side context, a capability we call prefill awareness. To do so, we construct a binary preference benchmark across three prefill mechanisms, filtering for cases where models show consistent stances. We find that frontier models show substantial prefill awareness: Claude Opus 4.5 detects prefills opposing its preferences in 9-35% of cases with a 0% false positive rate when prompted; additionally, models often revert towards baseline behavior without explicitly reporting that the prefill was foreign. Controlled ablations later also show that detection and resistance rely on different cues, where stylistic mismatch mainly affects whether models flag a prefill as foreign, while preference mismatch mainly affects whether they revert toward their baseline answer. We also examine more realistic agentic settings such as misalignment-continuation evaluations and SWE-bench trajectories, where frontier models sometimes disavow prefilled assistant turns in ways that depend strongly on dataset, task success, and hidden formatting artifacts. Our results indicate that prefill awareness is already a substantial confound for some prefill-based methods. We recommend that model developers track this capability in frontier systems.

</details>


### [179] [Constructing Evaluation Datasets for Procedural Reasoning: Balancing Naturalness, Grounding, and Multi-Hop Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12767)
*Sarah Elshabrawy,Rahul K. Dass,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 比较了三种基于TMK模型的问题生成策略，发现严格TMK生成策略在质量上表现最佳（96.5%问题有依据、92.6%问题可用），强调程序丰富性和自然表达不能保证表征依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI辅助学习系统中的程序推理需要既具有学习者特征又基于系统预期使用的教学知识的问答数据集，研究TMK问题生成策略如何影响程序和多跳推理的数据集质量。

Method: 比较三种生成策略：严格的TMK模型生成、先转录后TMK过滤的生成、结合转录与结构化指导的TMK感知生成；引入基于TMK模型提取的闭集证据单元的依据验证框架，评估答案是否受基础表征支持、问题是否自包含、是否针对多跳程序推理。

Result: 在23个教学主题和690个生成的问答对中，严格TMK生成实现最强整体质量（96.5%问题有依据，92.6%问题可用）；转录优先生成产生更多学习者式问题但更多依赖语境或依据薄弱的项目；TMK感知生成产生高原始多跳覆盖率但依据性较低。

Conclusion: 程序丰富性和自然表达不能保证表征依据，需要为AI辅助学习中的评估数据集进行显式的表征感知验证。

Abstract: Evaluating procedural reasoning in AI-supported learning systems requires question-answer datasets that are both learner-like and grounded in the instructional knowledge the system is expected to use. We study how TMK-based question generation strategies affect dataset quality for procedural and multi-hop reasoning.
  We compare three strategies: strict generation from Task-Method-Knowledge (TMK) models, transcript-first generation with post-hoc TMK filtering, and TMK-aware generation that combines transcripts with structured guidance. To evaluate generated items, we introduce a grounding validation framework based on closed-set evidence units extracted from TMK models. The framework measures whether answers are supported by the underlying representation, whether questions are self-contained, and whether they target multi-hop procedural reasoning.
  Across 23 instructional topics and 690 generated question-answer pairs, strict TMK generation achieves the strongest overall quality, with 96.5% grounded questions and 92.6% usable questions. Transcript-first generation produces more learner-like questions but more context-dependent or weakly grounded items, while TMK-aware generation yields high raw multi-hop coverage but lower grounding. These results show that procedural richness and natural phrasing do not guarantee representational grounding, motivating explicit representation-aware validation for evaluation datasets in AI-supported learning.

</details>


### [180] [A Tutorial on World Models and Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12783)
*Il-Seok Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一个关于世界建模的教程，统一了显式和隐式世界模型框架，为物理AI和AGI发展提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模正在成为构建具备预测、推理和决策能力的智能系统的核心原则。显式世界模型（学习结构化动态用于基于展开的推理与规划）和隐式世界模型（将预测结构编码于可扩展的学习表示中）这两种互补范式为机器人和自动驾驶等领域的物理AI奠定了基础，使智能系统能够超越现实约束下的反应式控制。

Method: 提出了一个连贯的框架，将多样化的世界建模方法通过共享的预测结构统一起来，并根据结构的表示和利用方式进行区分。该框架整合了显式和隐式两种互补范式，并指出近期的基础模型为统一感知、预测和行动的系统提供了一条路径。

Result: 建立了一个统一的世界建模框架，明确了显式和隐式世界模型之间的核心区别，以及它们如何通过共享的预测结构相互补充，为物理AI系统的设计提供了理论指导。

Conclusion: 尽管在物理AI领域取得了快速进展，但在分层推理、长程规划和自主目标形成等关键能力上仍面临重大挑战，这些能力对于向人工通用智能迈进至关重要。本教程为理解和推进世界建模研究提供了统一的视角和框架。

Abstract: World modeling is emerging as a central principle for building intelligent systems capable of prediction, reasoning, and decision making. A central distinction can be drawn between explicit world models, which learn structured dynamics for rollout-based reasoning and planning, and implicit world models, which encode predictive structure within scalable learned representations. These complementary paradigms provide a foundation for physical AI in domains such as robotics and autonomous driving, enabling intelligence beyond reactive control under real-world constraints. Recent foundation models further suggest a pathway toward unified systems integrating perception, prediction, and action. Despite rapid progress, major challenges remain in hierarchical reasoning, long-horizon planning, and autonomous goal formation, which are critical for advancing toward artificial general intelligence. This tutorial presents a coherent framework in which diverse world modeling approaches are unified through shared predictive structure and differentiated by how such structure is represented and exploited.

</details>


### [181] [The Containment Gap: How Deployed Agentic AI Frameworks Fail Public-Facing Safety Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12797)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Weiqi Liu,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了三个主流智能体框架（LangChain、AutoGPT、OpenAI Agents SDK），发现均未提供结构化安全保证，特别是缺乏内存完整性保护。实证显示针对LangChain构建的政府福利智能体的内存投毒攻击可将定向错误拒绝率提升至88.9%。作者提出两种轻量级遏制机制（内存完整性验证器和策略门），以亚毫秒开销消除了攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主调用工具、维护持久记忆并执行多步规划的智能体大语言模型系统日益部署于面向公众的高风险领域（如政府服务、医疗分诊、金融顾问），但当前用于构建这些系统的框架是否在架构层面提供结构化安全保证尚不明确，存在潜在安全隐患。

Method: 基于六项遏制原则（源自智能体架构的组合模型），对三个主流框架（LangChain、AutoGPT、OpenAI Agents SDK）进行架构级审计；在LangChain上构建模拟政府福利智能体进行实证验证，测试内存投毒攻击效果；提出并验证两种轻量级遏制机制的有效性与开销。

Result: 三个框架均无原生合规性，均未观察到内存完整性保护；针对LangChain智能体的单次内存投毒写入在所有测试种子和后端中诱导持续性定向腐败，将目标申请者的错误拒绝率提升至88.9%；在复杂五因素政策下，相同攻击保持整体准确率的同时将定向错误拒绝提升3.5倍，难以通过标准监控检测；所提机制以<0.2ms/调用的开销消除了两类攻击向量。

Conclusion: 当前智能体框架生态系统可能尚未满足面向公众部署的"默认安全"预期，需要在高风险、社会影响重大的应用中优先实施架构级干预措施，以实现可信部署。

Abstract: Agentic large language model systems that autonomously invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, and execute multi-step plans are increasingly deployed in public-facing domains, including government services, healthcare triage, and financial advising. We ask whether the frameworks used to build these systems provide architectural-level structural safety guarantees. Applying six containment principles derived from a compositional model of agentic architectures, we audit three dominant frameworks (LangChain, AutoGPT, and OpenAI Agents SDK) and find no native compliance in any of them. Memory integrity, a defense against one of the most prevalent vulnerability classes, is not observed in any of the three evaluated frameworks.
